# Interpretability audit across text, protein, and joint generative models

**Status:** active; canonical findings and decision record **Supersedes:** the exploratory contents of the retired repository-root `check.md`, to which the section-numbered `check.md §…` citations in the experiment and project logs still refer; repository-root `summary.md` is a non-canonical reader summary **Updated:** 2026-08-14; the claim register is current through EXP-R2-197, and `docs/EXPERIMENT_LOG.md` is the chronological register

---

## 0. Objective and Current Programme

1. **Compare model families from first principles.** Characterise differences in tokenization, training corpora, and model architecture among pure-text, pure-protein, and joint language–protein generative models. Prefer matched checkpoint lineages and same-checkpoint text/protein modes where they improve identification.
2. **Use and audit interpretability methods.** Apply interpretability methods as controlled measurements of behaviour, representation, and causal computation. Scope each limitation as method-intrinsic or transfer-specific, then localise transfer-specific limitations to model, training data or stage, modality, or evaluation interface.
3. **Develop and validate methods for biological knowledge.** Propose and validate interpretability methods that distinguish biological knowledge encoded by protein-generative models from corpus retrieval, interface artefacts, and surface correlations. Only methods that pass causal, retrieval-aware, and independent biological validation may be used to formulate and test new biological hypotheses.

The pure-text and pure-protein programme closed on 2026-08-06 is the inherited baseline, not the whole current objective. Its measurements, retractions, and stopped branches remain binding and are not rerun merely to fill a larger matrix. New joint-model, knowledge-source, and novelty work asks new questions and does not reinterpret the old panel by default.

### Forward tracks

| Item | Question | Admission and stopping rule |
|---|---|---|
| **D1.d — joint-model foundational bridge** | Which differences remain when text and protein modes share one checkpoint? | Qualify one dense joint decoder first: strict load, native rendering, scored positions, output semantics, and context-information checks for both modes. Same-checkpoint comparison controls shared weights and most architecture, not tokenization, prompts, output space, or training exposure. Add a joint MoE only if the dense result leaves routing or expert specialisation as a live explanation. **EXP-R2-151 first found no qualified bridge:** the two reachable dense joint decoders fail on opposite modes at identical architecture and scale—both `OPTForCausalLM` 24 × 2048 × 32. Context information, seeded draws, band 64–246: `galactica-1.3b` reads **+4.6439** on text and **+0.0481** on protein, so its protein mode is unmeasurable on this cohort; InstructProtein reads **+2.0490** on protein and **−15.9991** on text, placing a mean **96.3%** of its probability mass on its own twenty residue tokens at every scored text position, so its text mode is collapsed. The directional reversal control agrees: the checkpoint that reads sequence directionally pays **2.1199** nats to have it reversed, while the two that do not pay **0.0564** and **0.0901**. **EXP-R2-152 then established the bridge.** On the ProLLaMA three-point lineage—one architecture, one tokenizer, 32 × 4096 × 32—`ProLLaMA_Stage_1` and `ProLLaMA` are measurable in both modes. The lineage localises the text cost: the protein continued-pretraining stage spends **4.69 nats/token, 85% of the base model's text context information**, while instruction tuning costs a further 0.10. The arm's own training scripts show why the first stage is special: it fully retrains the embedding and output head, whereas the second is pure LoRA. **That correlation suggested the vocabulary interface, and the component swap refuted it.** EXP-R2-163 and EXP-R2-166 move the two checkpoints' tensors between each other — 291 tensors, verified `INTERCHANGEABLE`, tying read from the tensors rather than the config — and measure each chimera in this same estimand. Of the 4.69 nats, installing stage 1's whole retrained interface into the base body costs **1.9%**, and restoring the base's interface to stage 1's body recovers **14.4%**; the rest travels with the transformer body. The two directions do not sum, so the loss is not additively decomposable into interface and body and any "X% is in the embedding" statement is ill-posed for this lineage. **Within** the interface the split is clean and near-additive: the output head carries 13.8 of those 14.4 points and the fully retrained input embedding carries 1.1, in both directions. The protein side answers differently — stage 1's protein capability survives the base input embedding at +0.5330 against its own +0.5505, while installing the base output head takes the arm to `unmeasurable_on_this_cohort` — so the acquired capability is carried jointly by the body and the output head and does not require the retrained embedding. **"The acquired protein capability is destroyed" was stronger than that cell's own controls support, and is rescoped (EXP-R2-174).** The head-swap chimera is unmeasurable on context information and is nevertheless not indifferent to reading direction: its reversal cost is **+0.1302 and +0.1379** nats per residue across two cohort draws, against stage 1's own +0.1442 and +0.1489 and the base model's −0.0013 and +0.0118. What the base output head removes is the arm's measurability on this estimand, not every trace of the directional protein structure adaptation installed. One caveat governs every number here: the estimand is a next-token cross-entropy, which the output head parameterises directly, so it attributes the measured loss and does not rank component importance in general. The base model's reversal cost is **−0.0013** and the adapted stage's **+0.1442**, so directional protein structure is absent before adaptation and present after it. **The whole attribution was re-measured on a second cohort draw and it does not move (EXP-R2-174).** All eight cells ran again at `--cohort-draw-seed 20260812` against 20260728 — two windows of two *different* seeded permutations, sharing no record — and every conclusion above survives: the largest attribution share moves **0.43 percentage points**, the forward/backward asymmetry reads 7.4x against 7.6x, within-interface additivity holds again, no protein cell changes its verdict and all eight protein magnitudes move by under 0.017 nats. **Two draws are a comparison and not a distribution**: no interval, standard error or bootstrap may be quoted from the pair, because at K = 2 a shifted cell cannot be told from a noisy one. Protein magnitudes are per token at 1.533 residues/token and are not comparable with per-residue arms; the lineage's text comparison is exact. **A representational fact about the same lineage now arrives from R2.4's instrument, and it is a lineage result rather than a method one (EXP-R2-202, corrected 2026-08-14).** Measured at the per-layer feed-forward output, protein continued-pretraining **concentrates the text-mode activation spectrum at every interior depth**: `Llama-2-7b-hf` exceeds `ProLLaMA_Stage_1` in participation ratio at **28 of 28** interior layers, median ratio **35x** over a range of 2.5x to 180x, and in effective rank at 28 of 28 — and excluding the degenerate layers 0, 1, 30 and 31 *raises* both rather than producing them (participation-ratio medians 906.2 against 23.4, effective-rank medians 2,273.0 against 294.0). **Its magnitude depends heavily on the statistic and reporting only the largest would not be honest:** the same contrast reads 35x on the participation ratio, **7.7x** on effective rank and **1.24x** on `r99` (3,670 against 2,954), because the participation ratio is dominated by the head of the spectrum. So the defensible statement is the concentration at every interior depth, strongest in the leading directions; "a 40x collapse" is the participation-ratio framing and does not travel alone. The dictionaries fitted at the same site show the same band from the other end — `base/text` holds 7,337 to 8,160 live latents per layer through layers 5–14 where `stage1/text` holds 2,597 to 3,907. **Whether this costs the text side anything behaviourally is not measured**, and the spectrum measurement's controls, thresholds and bounds are recorded once, in §8 item 5. **A second reading of the same spectra says where the two checkpoints put their dimension, and it needs no dictionary at all, which is what makes it a statement about the models.** In protein mode the pre-adaptation checkpoint's effective dimension peaks **early and declines with depth** — 3,409 at layer 3, falling steadily to about 1,900 by layer 29, Spearman(layer, `r99`) **−0.544** — while the adapted checkpoint's **rises to a mid-stack plateau**, peaking across a broad tie region spanning layers **17–22** (maximum 3,169 at layer 18; the argmax alone is not identified, Appendix B rule 35), and then collapses at the top to 939, 606 and 87 at layers 29, 30 and 31. **The lineage's third checkpoint localises this to one stage.** `ProLLaMA` — instruction tuning on top of `ProLLaMA_Stage_1`, measured on the same site, cohort and pin — reproduces the adapted profile rather than extending it: maximum again at layer 18, tie region **17–22**, prominence over its own interior median 1.162 against Stage_1's 1.150. **The relocation belongs to the continued-pretraining stage and does not continue at the instruction stage.** In text the three cells read 1.009, 1.170 and 1.096. **All of these are calibrated against a null and that is what makes them readable**: the `coordinate_independent_control` recorded per layer by the same stage returns prominence excesses of 0.0014–0.0038, so the protein cells clear it by 48x–111x and the two adapted text cells by 41x–44x. `base/text` clears it by only **6.3x**, and 2.2x against the largest null excess in the set, so its depth structure is **too weak to characterise** rather than absent — the earlier reading of this contrast as presence versus absence is withdrawn, and what survives is that the base carries an order of magnitude less prominence than either adapted checkpoint, 0.009 of excess against 0.170 and 0.096. The difference between those two adapted text values is itself unpriced: one control draw per cell prices the null's centre and not the sampling spread of a structured curve. **Note the standing of this result: no interpretability method is in the loop.** It is measured on activations directly, with no dictionary, probe or lens anywhere in it, so it is not exposed to the faithfulness questions that qualify most of this document. So protein continued-pretraining does not merely concentrate this lineage's spectra, it moves where along the stack the dimension sits, and the instruction stage that follows leaves the protein profile where it found it. The bounds of the concentration result travel unchanged, and one more belongs to this reading: the two modes are two corpora, and a depth profile is a property of the site measured, which is one module's output and not the residual stream. Qualification admits an arm to measurement and asserts nothing about what it knows. |
| **D1.e — knowledge source and novelty** | Does protein fitness or generation exceed what homologous training examples provide? | EXP-R2-143 supplied the pure-model retrieval bound in F10, whose irreducible limitation was that retrieval could only ever be *estimated* from a corpus proxy and subtracted, never excluded, because every natural protein has homologues in the corpus. **EXP-R2-189 measured how absolutely that binds and closed the two routes this row used to name.** Over F10's own 187 wild types the least homologous is still **55.5%** identical to a staged UniRef50 cluster and none falls below 55%, so a family-disjoint natural referent does not exist in this benchmark; a temporal split against ProtGPT2's UniRef50 2021_04 cutoff yields **one** post-cutoff wild type against an eight-unit floor, and a curation date is an upper bound on novelty in any case. The only class disjoint by construction is non-natural sequence. **That referent now exists and is certified, and the certificate is the instrument this track was missing (EXP-R2-190).** Every wild type of one MegaScale file carrying an explicit wild-type row — a census, not a sample — was searched against the staged 60.3 M-cluster UniRef50 at F10's own `homology_control` gate: **132 of 148 de novo designs return no hit**, against a within-cohort natural control from the same file, the same assay technology and matched length that hits at **328 of 330, 99.39%**, an ordering invariant across four orders of magnitude of e-value and at every identity threshold. **The 16 hits are identified and are not natural homology** — every one is to a UniParc-derived cluster and none to a named UniProt entry, thirteen of them to the *same* cluster, and one design is verbatim in UniRef50 at 100% identity over all 43 residues — so what is excluded on those four series is design-campaign self-homology surfacing through a single deposited member, which makes the removal a named, auditable exclusion rather than a haircut across all 41 series. **The length difference runs toward false absence and is measured rather than argued:** designs are shorter than the naturals (median 43 against 59) and a shorter query is harder to align, yet in the 40–47 band holding 133 of the 148 designs the natural control still hits at **30 of 31, 96.77%**. Absence is certified against the searched snapshot alone; ProtGPT2's UniRef50 2021_04 is 17% smaller, so absence from the newer implies absence from the older and the bias runs safe, while every ProGen2 rung also saw BFD30, which was not searched. **The scale at which the certificate excludes has since been measured, and it qualifies the instrument rather than withdrawing it (EXP-R2-196).** Disjointness of *alignable sequence* is real, and it is precisely what no natural referent in this benchmark could offer. Disjointness of corpus *information* is not what it delivers, and the gap is not marginal: at fragment orders 3 through 6 **not one** fragment of any scored variant of the 130 certified designs is absent from the searched corpus — zero unseen of 4,427,168 full-order positions at order 6, where the natural-domain control has four — so at fragment scale the corpus-disjoint referent is *more* completely covered than the natural domains. What the certificate buys, and what nothing drawn from natural protein could, is the exclusion of **whole-sequence retrieval**, which is exactly the quantity F10 could only estimate and subtract. **The question therefore has its first answer that rests on no corpus proxy, and that answer is F12**: with retrieval excluded by construction rather than estimated and subtracted, ProtGPT2's prediction of measured folding stability exceeds the corpus's own fragment statistics and does not exceed a free hydrophobicity scale. The standing requirements are otherwise unchanged. A next claim needs disjoint data, per-arm corpus identification, and external phenotype or function; alignment-free is not information-free, so a fragment-level baseline is mandatory wherever a profile lookup is empty by construction; and a model score, predicted structure, or linguistic description alone cannot establish novel function. |
| **D2.h — joint-model method-fidelity bridge** | Do the measured PAA and replacement failures persist within shared weights? | Use the same interface → measurable target → attainability/power → behaviour → causal target → independent-data order as the pure-model audit. Select only the cells needed for one declared hypothesis; do not rerun the old method menu or build a model × method Cartesian product. Replacement training begins only when cheaper measurements leave a fidelity question open. **One admissibility rule is now fixed and binds every cell here (L29): on a joint checkpoint the component grid is the block family alone, so a joint-versus-dense replacement comparison is not admissible and no modality claim may rest on it, while the within-checkpoint text-versus-protein comparison is admissible because both sides carry the identical grid and the asymmetry cancels.** **The first replacement measurement on a joint checkpoint has since run, and it is recorded as a measurement rather than interpreted (EXP-R2-182).** **Its joint-checkpoint reading is WITHDRAWN as of 2026-08-14, in text mode, on the criterion frozen before the comparison ran (EXP-R2-194): the pre-adaptation checkpoint recovers negative under the same recipe, so what is withdrawn is the attribution of these numbers to the adapted weights and not the measurement, which stands. The last block of this row is the record of the withdrawal, and the struck words further down mark where the withdrawn attribution was written.** Four cells on `ProLLaMA_Stage_1` — two modes at two seeds, per-layer transcoders at `d_hidden` 8192 and k 32, 128 sequences — all reached their end. **Behavioural recovery is negative in all four**: protein **−0.4617** and **−0.2266**, text **−0.1250** and **−0.1050**. Splicing the trained dictionary in damages the model *more* than deleting the block's contribution entirely and substituting its per-layer mean. Every verdict is PARTIAL, every attainability is ATTAINABLE at a ceiling above 0.99, and every causal gate FAILs. **No reading of it is recorded**: the candidates — a train/eval population gap, the sequential-replacement convention against the teacher-forced convention the dictionaries were fitted under, and dictionaries undertrained at this budget — are not separated by anything measured, so nothing here concludes anything about joint-model replaceability. Nothing about F11 may be read from it either: at 2x expansion on a 32 × 4096 backbone these absolute numbers are not comparable with the panel's 12x results, and a *negative* text recovery is the first thing that would have to be understood before the protein side meant anything. **The follow-up depth sweep makes that measurement more specific without resolving it (EXP-R2-186, with resampling intervals added in EXP-R2-187).** The same four cells were re-scored with only the first 1, 2, 4, 8, 16 or 32 blocks replaced, on the same dictionaries and the same cohort, every front sharing the batches and both endpoints; the 32-block row reproduces the headline replacement bit-exactly, which is what makes a shallower front commensurable with it. **At a one-block front recovery is 0.87 to 0.96 in every cell**, so the damage behind the negative headline is absent from a single block's substitution and is a property of composition. **The depth curve is then not monotone in any cell**: six adjacent steps recover *more* when *more* blocks are replaced, which pure error compounding cannot produce, so compounding is neither excluded nor established. **All six reversals survive resampling, so the non-monotonicity stands on four cells and both modes rather than on the two text cells alone (EXP-R2-187).** The separation criterion was fixed before any interval existed: a step counts as a real increase only when the 95% percentile interval of the *paired* difference lies wholly above zero, and an interval covering zero is reported UNRESOLVED, which says this cohort cannot separate the step from zero and never that the step is absent. Nothing returned UNRESOLVED and all fourteen falling steps separate below zero, so the instrument resolves in both directions; the widest reversal, one text cell from an 8-block front to the full 32, is **+0.4669 [+0.3997, +0.5337]**. The estimator is the stage's existing paired construction at its established replicate count and unit floor, and the deepest row's interval is identical digit for digit to the headline's own. **EXP-R2-186's own bounds paragraph had guessed the other way** — that the small protein reversals "cannot be separated from noise" — and it is superseded by measurement rather than quietly dropped; the reason is the pairing, since six manipulations of one cohort leave most per-sequence variability cancelling in the difference. **Three qualifications hold the result at its size.** A separated interval bounds the **sign, not the importance**: the smallest surviving step is **+0.0088** against a total fall of about 1.3, real and negligible at once, and reading "survives resampling" as "matters" is the error this table most invites. The finding does not hinge on the statistic except at that step, since under the weaker marginal reading — non-overlap of the two fronts' own intervals — five of the six still separate. And it **adjudicates no cause**: the fitted-versus-scored convention candidate remains neither removed nor established, the reconstruction-NMSE follow-up was not run, and the standing conclusion is still negative recovery in all four cells with the candidates unseparated. Six fronts over 32 layers sample the depth curve rather than resolve it, a front is always a prefix so nothing here says *which* layers carry the damage, and the bootstrap prices cohort-sampling noise alone — the two dictionary seeds are two cells, not a resampling of a seed population. **The scope of that non-monotonicity is now measured rather than assumed (EXP-R2-188).** The same sweep was repeated on the panel arms where the replacement gate carries claims — gpt2-large and ProtGPT2 as the architecturally matched primary pair, 36 blocks by 1280 at the same 12x dictionary width, so a front is the same prefix depth on both — with gpt2 and ZymCTRL declared secondary in advance and unable to change the licence. Every deepest front reproduces its published headline bit-exactly, and each new artefact's whole gates block differs from that arm's 2026-08-10 cell in not one numeric value, so these are the published measurements re-scored at shallower depths. **Over all 73 ordered pairs of fronts across the four cells there is not one separated increase**: 72 separate below zero and one is UNRESOLVED. All 21 of the text control's pairs are DECREASE, which discharges the pre-declared attainability condition and makes the protein verdicts readable, and its resolution is what makes the null worth something — it separates an adjacent step of **−0.000133** at an interval half-width of 0.000079, sixty-six times smaller than the smallest reversal that survived resampling on the joint checkpoint and about 2,500 times smaller than the largest of the six. **Non-monotonicity is therefore a property of this joint checkpoint and its 2x dictionary family rather than of prefix replacement as a method, and it earns no limitations-catalogue entry.** It is **not** a refutation of EXP-R2-186 or EXP-R2-187, whose four cells stand on their own intervals; what is now known is only that the phenomenon does not travel to the panel arms at this cohort size. **One step is unresolved and is not rounded away**: ProtGPT2's shallowest step has a *positive* point estimate of **+0.004957** with 87.6% of replicates positive and an interval covering zero, so that arm is not a clean monotone cell, and its half-width there is about 1.8 times wider than the interval that resolved the joint checkpoint's smallest reversal, so it cannot exclude a reversal of that size — unresolved, never absent. **What does travel is the other half, and it is the portable statement in this series**: at a one-block front recovery runs **0.932 to 0.9998** on all four panel arms against 0.87 to 0.96 on the joint cells, so the damage being a property of composition rather than of any single block's reconstruction now holds on eight cells over five checkpoints and both modalities. **No cause is adjudicated here either**, and one inference is refused explicitly — recovery stays positive at every front on the panel arms and crosses zero on the joint checkpoint, but that checkpoint differs in backbone, in expansion (2x against 12x), in dictionary sparsity, in training budget and in being joint at all, and this run varies none of them, so the turn may not be attributed to the negative-recovery regime. Two conditions travel on the face of every artefact: the grid carries the block family alone, so these four cells are the within-checkpoint comparison and not a dense comparison; and the pair certifies `MATCHED` with `max_tokens [1024, 512]` and `max_tokens_agree false`, so **no verdict here certifies equal context**. **The shortlist of candidate explanations for those four negative cells has since lost its most mundane member (EXP-R2-195 pre-declared, EXP-R2-197 read).** Those cells ran at 2x expansion while every positive panel result runs at 12x, and width had never been varied. Swept on `gpt2` at 2x, 4x, 8x and 12x with `k`, step count, batch size, corpus, both seeds and every other matched field held — all six pairs certifying `disagreements` exactly `['d_hidden']` at an identical realised token count, and all four cells returning the identical clean and fully-ablated endpoints to twelve decimals, which is what makes them four widths of one comparison — **recovery is POSITIVE at every width: +0.8358, +0.8751, +0.8997, +0.9088**, every interval far above zero and the curve monotone in width. Nothing is marginal about it: at the narrowest the replacement sits **5.31 nats below** the mean-ablation endpoint, and its interval's lower bound is about a hundred half-widths above zero. **Undertraining cannot rescue the hypothesis, and the direction was declared before the run**: at a fixed token budget a narrow dictionary is *better* trained per latent, 3,959 tokens per latent at 2x against 660 at 12x, so **2x is the best-trained dictionary in the sweep and still the worst performer** — its lower recovery is a width effect and not a data effect. The reproduction anchor holds, which is what makes the rest readable: the freshly trained 12x cell lands **0.000358** from this arm's published F11 value, inside the 0.0007 that separates the arm's own two published dictionary seeds and far inside the pre-declared 0.02 void threshold. **So width is removed as a *sufficient* explanation of ~~the joint checkpoint's~~ these cells' negative sign, and this is a useful negative rather than a failed experiment** — a negative recovery produced by this recipe on a *text control* would have been a property of the method, and no width produces one. **What removes it as a sufficient cause does not remove it as one term of an interaction, and one arithmetic point bounds the transfer more sharply than "different backbone" does**: "2x expansion" is not one recipe across backbones. At `d_model` 4096 with `k` 32 the joint cells' 2x dictionary runs at an active fraction of **0.391%**, against **4.167%** for this sweep's 2x cell and **0.694%** for even its widest; on that axis the joint cells sit outside the swept range entirely rather than at its narrow end. `k` is fixed at 64 throughout, so expansion and active fraction are inversely coupled here by construction and the 2x-with-`k`-32 combination those cells actually ran is untested. **This settles no joint attribution**: the frozen base-versus-adapted comparison that would is pre-declared and ~~paused on the errored cluster~~ **has since run on the repaired one, with the result at the end of this row**, and neither may be read as having settled the other. Two earlier readings extend to every width and are recorded here rather than re-argued: **all forty ordered front pairs across the four cells return DECREASE**, so EXP-R2-188's monotone depth result on this arm is width-invariant and the non-monotonicity stays a property of the joint checkpoint; and one-block recovery runs **0.9949 to 0.9983** across the four widths, so the portable statement that the damage is a property of composition now holds at four expansion factors as well as across five checkpoints. The sweep ran on the **local L20 workstation under an explicit and temporary user authorisation while the H200 cluster is errored** — a deliberate departure from the standing rule reserving the L20 for correctness checks, scoped to a 124 M-parameter arm and not a precedent for local panel campaigns — and cross-platform recovery differences below about 1e-3 are not resolvable against any published H200 number, which is why the anchor is quoted as a bound rather than as a reproduction to more digits than it holds. **The frozen base-versus-adapted comparison has since run on the repaired cluster, and the branch that fired was pre-declared as forcing a withdrawal rather than a qualification (EXP-R2-194, read 2026-08-14).** Three cells were scored — the pre-adaptation text cell, the adapted text cell and an adapted protein cell — over R2.4's independent dictionaries, trained at one recipe, one corpus, one seed and one held-out draw on `Llama-2-7b-hf` and on `ProLLaMA_Stage_1`, on a snapshot verified against the commit that produced EXP-R2-182 and EXP-R2-186 to 188; the two text cells carry the same cohort digest and the same scoring-budget digest and differ in the backbone alone, and both are ATTAINABLE at ceilings above 0.99. **Both text cells recover negative** — pre-adaptation **−0.1149 [−0.1201, −0.1098]**, adapted **−0.1386 [−0.1490, −0.1285]** — so the pre-declared RECIPE branch fires and **the joint-checkpoint reading of EXP-R2-182 is withdrawn, in text mode, rather than qualified: the same recipe on the same backbone fails in the same direction before any protein pretraining has occurred, so those four cells may no longer be read as a fact about the adapted checkpoint.** **What is withdrawn is the attribution and not the measurement**: the four cells stand as measured, and the adapted protein cell re-scored here at **−0.4096**, on endpoints bit-identical to EXP-R2-182's protein cells over the same protein cohort — clean, fully ablated and denominator to every reported digit, with only the replacement term differing — makes the adapted checkpoint's protein negative a three-seed result. **The withdrawal is text-mode because the pre-adaptation protein mode is behaviourally unmeasurable**: no base-side protein recovery number exists, no cross-checkpoint protein contrast can be formed, and the base protein cell was deliberately not scored rather than scored into a complete-looking artefact whose behavioural numbers must not be read — so on the protein side nothing localises the sign in either direction. These two text cells are matched to each other and **not** to EXP-R2-182's, which ran at a 512-token cap and different dictionary seeds. **The ratio and the numerator point opposite ways, and a reader given only the ratio would take away the opposite impression.** The two text denominators differ 2.2-fold (8.3356 against 3.7913): on the ratio the adapted cell looks worse by 0.0237, while measured as excess damage beyond full mean ablation the **pre-adaptation** cell is damaged nearly twice as much, **+0.9577 nats per token against +0.5255**. The intervals do separate, so the adaptation adds a small further decrement to the ratio — but it adds it to a failure that is already there, and on the unnormalised scale the pre-adaptation cell is the worse of the two. The numerator strengthens the withdrawal rather than weakening it, and the ratio is not to be quoted alone. **The same run dissociates two phenomena this row had carried together, and the dissociation points the other way from the withdrawal.** Over the 15 ordered pairs of fronts per cell the pre-adaptation cell is **strictly monotone — 15 DECREASE, 0 INCREASE, 0 UNRESOLVED** — falling from **+0.9998** at a one-block front to −0.1149 at thirty-two and separating every one of its pairs, so its monotonicity is a measured null and not an instrument too coarse to see a reversal; the two adapted cells are not monotone, at **6/7/2** and **12/2/1**. **So the negative sign is present before adaptation and is a property of the recipe, while the non-monotonicity is absent before adaptation and, on this evidence, belongs to the adaptation.** That is the pre-adaptation control EXP-R2-186 to 188 explicitly lacked, and the two localisations are not to be collapsed into one. It rests on **one** base cell, one lineage and text mode, and nothing here says why. **The portable statement gains a checkpoint as well**: the pre-adaptation cell's **+0.9998** at a one-block front joins the 0.932–0.9998 band of the four panel arms and the 0.87–0.96 of the joint cells, so the damage being a property of composition rather than of a single block's reconstruction now holds on a pre-adaptation 7B checkpoint too. **What is now excluded as a *sufficient* cause of the negative sign, and how the standing conclusion is to be worded.** Tested and excluded: single-block reconstruction (the one-block front, now on six checkpoints), compounding as the whole story (the reversals), the train/eval population gap (EXP-R2-191's cohort-against-held-out reconstruction ratios of 0.904 to 1.018), protein-side undertraining (the held-out plateau by step 12,000), dictionary width across 2x to 12x (EXP-R2-197), and now **the adaptation itself**. What is left is the **fitted-versus-scored convention mismatch**, and it is to be stated as *the last candidate standing after every other has been tested somewhere* rather than as the remaining item on a list of untested guesses — which is a claim about the state of the search and **not** a claim that the convention mismatch is the cause, for which there is still no evidence of any kind. Bounds: one seed per text cell against three on the protein side, one cohort at one draw with no skip-offset condition, one expansion factor at 2x, six fronts on 32 blocks with a front always a prefix so nothing says *which* blocks carry the damage, and a text-mode attribution throughout. |
| **D3 — methods for biological knowledge and discovery** | Can an interpretable and causally faithful measurement distinguish learned biological knowledge from retrieval or surface correlation, then produce a testable biological hypothesis? | D3.g, D3.h, and D3.i remain gated candidates; D3.e and D3.f remain closed, and **D3.b closed on 2026-08-12 (EXP-R2-173, EXP-R2-178) with its motivating premise refuted on its own arms**. **D3.d was re-chartered to run independent of D3.c and then halted at its own pre-registered refusal on the same day** (EXP-R2-176 to 184): its corpus-coupling baseline fails its own positive control on 22 of 22 proteins, so the second-order subtraction the item was admitted for cannot be built on this interface, and no downstream number is interpreted. A proposal must answer a declared biological knowledge question or a reproducible measurement failure. A joint checkpoint provides a controlled bridge, but shared weights do not define a cross-modal target or relax the requirements for real sequence–description pairs, calibrated concepts, causal intervention, retrieval controls, and external biological evidence. |

EXP-R2-150 was a bounded robustness check on the declared sequence band for existing protein replacement results. **It is complete (2026-08-10)** and did not reopen the completed pure-model panel: ZymCTRL's absolute recovery is a property of the arm rather than of the band, EXP-R2-135's 4.1× band dependence is substantially a property of the released ProGen3 dictionary rather than of the estimand, and the modality separation is band-robust. Its consequence for wording is recorded in F11.

Section 9 preserves the plan that produced the inherited baseline, including its identifiers, costs, closed branches, and preregistrations. It is an evidence record; the forward tracks above govern new work, and `docs/RESEARCH_PLAN.md` translates them into an executable comparison and stage guide.

### Priority order, revised 2026-08-10 after a literature review

Forty-one papers and twenty-three repositories were reviewed against these tracks by six independent readers, and their claims were arbitrated against our own artefacts rather than adopted. The review confirmed F10 from three independent directions, confirmed EXP-R2-151 from Galactica's own reported perplexities, corrected EXP-R2-152's mechanism, put half of the induction claim at risk (EXP-R2-155), and proposed one alternative explanation of F11 that our own artefacts refute (EXP-R2-156). What it changed most is the **order** of work: the cheapest decisive experiments are now training-free controls we had not run, not new campaigns.

| # | Experiment | Answers | Why it is ranked here |
|---|---|---|---|
| **1** | ~~**Neuron-basis baseline.** Circuit tracing on raw MLP **pre-down-projection** activations, no dictionary trained, on the architecture-matched non-gated triple gpt2-large / ProtGPT2 / ZymCTRL.~~ **Run 2026-08-10 (EXP-R2-157)**, and re-run the same day under an attribution score matched to the intervention it approximates. Its outcome and bounds are the log entry's; nothing from it has entered §1's register, so it is quoted as a measurement and not as a claim. | F11's proximal cause | The only single experiment that **discriminates** the two live hypotheses: if neurons work on protein, the failure is the learned dictionary and is method-attributable; if neurons fail as badly, protein MLP computation is genuinely dense and no sparse basis will recover it, which is a model-property finding. Training-free. Two independent readers converged on it. **Must use the pre-down-projection tensor** — the post-down-projection one is far less sparse and would manufacture a false "proteins need dictionaries" conclusion. |
| **2** | ~~**Matched-perturbation null.** Perturb each MLP output by a random vector matched to the transcoder's own error in **both norm and angle**; report ΔCE and KL beside the dictionary's.~~ **Run 2026-08-10 (EXP-R2-158).** The perturbation-tolerance reading it recorded beside its own answer was then qualified by EXP-R2-159 to EXP-R2-162, so those five entries are read together and not separately; none of them has entered §1's register. | whether the error is large or *pathologically directed* | EXP-R2-156 established that protein absolute damage is 8–17x the text arms' per symbol. This says whether that damage is what any perturbation of that size would do, or whether the protein dictionary's error is aimed at the load-bearing subspace. One extra forward pass per arm, no training. |
| **3** | ~~**Collision null for the induction census.** Score the same heads on length- and composition-matched probes carrying no planted repeat; read each arm against its own null rather than against uniform attention.~~ **Run 2026-08-12 (EXP-R2-171, five entries).** Its own statistic is vetoed by its own specificity control and retired: of four successive floors, three fail that control outright and the fourth's version of it is vacuous by construction. The question it was built to settle is answered anyway, by the census it was calibrating: a byte-level **text** decoder falls inside the protein range on the uncorrected published statistic, so the panel-level ordering is refuted rather than corrected. §4 carries the result, the identity ceilings it contributes, and the retirement of the count it was built on. | closes the risk EXP-R2-155 opened | The panel-level half of the induction claim is now retired permanently rather than pending. Forward passes only. |
| **4** | **Datapoint-feature diagnostic.** Measure near-duplicate rate in each transcoder's training stream and test for family-memorisation latents. | an untested mechanism for F11 | Repetition x sparsity produces dictionaries that are monosemantic, low-MSE and mechanistically wrong — our exact signature. ZymCTRL's stream is already measured at 20.2% exact duplicates. CPU-only to diagnose; Jacobian matching is the candidate fix if confirmed. |
| **5** | **Vocabulary-interface test for the joint text cost.** Qualify a checkpoint whose residues occupy a token set disjoint from text while text ids stay identical to its base. | tests EXP-R2-152's corrected mechanism | The correction localised the cost to the stage that retrains the embedding and output head. This is the one design that separates *vocabulary-interface interference* from *parameter interference*, and it is a prediction our own correction makes. |
| **6** | **Same-input Model Diffing Crosscoder (D3.h).** Compare matched ProLLaMA stages separately within each mode, only after exact input, position, layer, and activation-site correspondence is established. **Both modes' baselines now exist (EXP-R2-175):** the protein cell was blocked by a split guard that was right, and what unblocked it is L30. **The independent-dictionary step has since finished, and ~~its pre-registered basis-adequacy gate now blocks the protein side~~ both of its pre-registered adequacy criteria are void on their own text control (EXP-R2-191, EXP-R2-194, EXP-R2-203):** the protein dictionaries' live bases do fall short, but the reading that a diff over them would compare two fitted bases rather than two models was licensed by a gate the control passes, and the control does not pass it — so that attribution is withdrawn while its measurement stands, and the successor rule is per-layer admissibility against the activations' own effective dimension (EXP-R2-202). | which stable features protein adaptation introduces, removes, or preserves | The base protein mode is only an adaptation reference; it cannot carry a knowledge claim. Simpler baselines precede Crosscoder training, and reconstruction alone stops at a representation result. Both baseline readings are representational and the stage declines an R2.4 decision by design. Full admission, stopping rule and the two-mode reading: §8. |
| **7** | **Sequence–description causal concept alignment (D3.g).** Use genuine protein-sequence–curated-description pairs and sequence- or declared concept-level units. | whether a calibrated cross-modal concept bridge exists and carries biological knowledge | Text and protein token positions are not paired, even inside one checkpoint; direct token-level Crosscoders remain inadmissible. Full admission and stopping rule: §8. |
| **8** | **Fixed-depth width ladder on the protein side.** | whether capacity is excluded a second time on a clean design | The existing protein ladder confounds depth with width, so no protein result can currently separate them. Requires admitting a new architecture family and is ranked below the training-free work for that reason. |
| **9** | **Retrieval decomposition at fixed weights.** Sweep a retrieval mixing weight against a homology ceiling on the one released model whose retrieval prior is provably a per-position profile. | restates F10 as a decomposition | Cheap and it sharpens a citable claim, but F10 already stands; this improves its form, not its status. |

**Where this order stands, 2026-08-12.** The ranking is unchanged and its top has moved: items 1, 2 and 3 are run, and items 4 to 9 stand as ranked. The rows carry the pointers into `docs/EXPERIMENT_LOG.md`; §1 remains the authority on what any of it licenses. Item 3 is the only one of the three to have reached §1 and §4, and it reached them as a retirement rather than as a claim. **One further measurement of item 2's instrument is recorded here so that its artefacts are not misread: a four-rung ProGen2 perturbation-tolerance ladder was run and licenses no scale claim** (EXP-R2-168, re-measured under the content-mask correction as EXP-R2-183). Its recovery ordering is stable across three conditions, two cohort draws and the correction — and it is **not monotone in parameter count**, ProGen2-medium at 765M being more tolerant than ProGen2-large at 2.78B at every magnitude measured. Depth and width co-vary across the rungs, so even a clean ordering would not have isolated scale, and this lineage's perturbation target is a parallel block's feed-forward output rather than the sequential block output the GPT-2 arms use. No scale claim is recorded and none may be read off those artefacts.

**Gated and unranked:** **D3.i, cross-modal causal circuit translation.** It remains on the pending list but receives zero implementation or compute until D3.g establishes a stable concept bridge and both a biologically grounded protein circuit and a language-side circuit pass their own causal-faithfulness gates. Full admission and stopping rule: §8.

**Closed or declined by this review, recorded so they are not re-proposed.** Distributed alignment search stays closed and its *reason* is upgraded: the vacuity theorem's assumptions are closer to satisfied on a small-vocabulary protein arm than on a text arm, so the protein side is the worst place to run it. The mixture-of-experts branch stays gated — two external papers corroborate our null without testing the function-class claim it was built on, and our dense-decoder control was already dispositive. No text MoE arm is earned. ESM3 is refused as a panel arm on verified code: bidirectional attention and entropy-ordered iterative unmasking, so it is not an autoregressive decoder. Of eight candidate tooling repositories none is adopted as a dependency — each demands a substrate swap for a small method core, and our per-architecture registries refuse an undeclared architecture where every candidate would resolve it silently. Two components are worth vendoring at roughly fifty lines total: an input-invariant transcoder decomposition, and a faithfulness curve marginalised over circuit size to replace a single-point ratio.

## 0.05 Retraction — the homology / memorisation control (EXP-R2-061)

> **Resolved by EXP-R2-064 (2026-07-29).** The search was re-run against the full UniRef50 snapshot with `--masking 0`, CPU only. Record 0 now returns 100% identity over all 732 residues and bins `ge95_near_duplicate`, so the fix took. The outcome splits by statistic. **Head count — the quantity the finding claims — survives and is reinstated**: no stratum separation on any arm, overlapping intervals everywhere, and the unmemorisable synthetic probe recruits as many heads as the near-duplicate stratum or more. **Peak prefix-matching strength reverses**: the pre-stated rule now returns `consistent_with_memorisation` on all three arms, ProtGPT2 having moved from `indeterminate` — reported as a reversal, though the repeat-length confound and EXP-R2-049 had already put that statistic out of use. The `lt30_no_detectable_homology` stratum turned out not to exist: all four of its members were truncated alignments, two of them verbatim corpus entries at 100% identity. The ZymCTRL falsification is untouched. Artefacts under `results/transfer_20260729/`; the retracted ones are kept as the evidence for this retraction. The paragraphs below stand as the record of the defect.

**`EXP-R2-050 "the induction head count is not memorisation" moves from *stands* to *retracted pending re-run*.**

`homology.run_diamond_blastp` built its DIAMOND command **without `--masking`**, so DIAMOND 2.1's default repeat masking was active — against a cohort **selected for internal tandem repeats**. The HSP stops at the repeat, `nident` under-counts, and `identity_over_query = nident/qlen` is not the query coverage its docstring claims.

Verified against the corpus rather than inferred: cohort record 0 is **byte-identical to `UniRef50_Q3E8Z8` over all 732 residues**, yet DIAMOND reported `pident 100, nident 607, qend 607, slen 732` → 82.9% → binned `id70_to_95_close_homology`. **A verbatim member of ProtGPT2's pretraining corpus was recorded as a diverged relative.** Five of forty-eight exact-cohort records are affected, all in the same direction, and all into the bin carrying the *highest* measured induction — i.e. the error works to defeat the memorisation hypothesis it was meant to test.

Compounding it: `bootstrap_stratum` had no unit floor. At n=4 a percentile interval is *pinched inward* — 9–15% relative width against 27% at n=420 in the same file — and two `consistent_with_memorisation` verdicts were decided by non-overlap of a four-unit interval against a four-hundred-unit one.

Re-run is `--stages search` only, **no GPU**. Note that `assign_homology` now raises on a truncated alignment by design, so re-running `--stages assign` over the existing masked TSV will stop rather than silently reproduce the old bins.

**Two further published numbers are now known to rest on file-order draws** and must be re-derived before citation:

- **L9's explanation-channel figures** (7.32 / 3.61 / 0.74 bits/symbol) — the stage takes an accession-order prefix while neighbouring modules permute. The caveat previously recorded against these numbers is now actionable, and a seeded sampler exists. **Discharged (EXP-R2-068, plan item B3): the re-derivation under seeded permutation is in §5.1's L9 block, which is the citable form; the three figures above are the superseded file-order draw.**
- **EXP-R2-040 probe and erasure numbers** — grouping units took corpus prefixes. Measured on the full EC corpus: **135 distinct grouping units under file order against 231 under a seeded permutation** of the same 400 proteins, with the EC-1 class share more than doubled. Separately, `family_disjoint=True` was asserted for `fitness` while ProteinGym ships four `BLAT_ECOLX` assays of a single 286-residue protein, and the behaviour cohort was the first N held-out units — for fitness, **forty mutants of one protein**.

  > **Scoped 2026-08-12, because as written this bullet covers L16 and nothing said so where L16 is quoted.** The re-derivation requirement is about *which units were drawn*, so it reaches every EXP-R2-040 number whose value depends on the grouping draw or on the `fitness` cohort. It does **not** reach the two cells L16 rests on, and that was checked against the retained artefacts rather than assumed: of the 26 erasure cells in `results/transfer_20260728/probe_erasure/`, exactly **three** are non-informative by the stage's own gate — ProtGPT2 `ec_class` (0.0781), and ZymCTRL `fitness` under both the fixed and the unconditioned tags (0.0559, 0.0708), all below the 0.10 clean-readability floor — and **no section of this document cites any of the three**. L16's two cells are ProtGPT2 `pfam_family` at a clean linear skill of **+0.7054** and ProGen2-medium `ss3` at **+0.2603**, both far above that floor, with erasure costs of −0.1787 and +0.0309 nats. So this is a citation-consistency problem and not a contamination: L16 stands as quoted in §5.1, which now carries the same scoping note, and the bullet governs the remaining EXP-R2-040 material.

And one interval is renamed rather than deleted: the **path-patching matched-pair result** (+0.020, CI [−0.121, +0.166]) resampled an arm's induction *heads* — its entire population, selected by a threshold — while the probe records, the only real sampling unit, contributed nothing. It measures heterogeneity, not sampling error. The point estimate stands; the interval does not mean what it said.

> **Extended by EXP-R2-068.** A unit floor now applies to that head resampling, at the eight units `homology.py` has enforced since EXP-R2-061. The headline above is unaffected, though not for the reason first written: its two-sample bootstrap resamples **38** heads (14 against 24), 14 being only the smaller side, and it carries `excludes_zero: false` — it was never a separation verdict, so there was nothing for the floor to withdraw. The two nodes that *are* separation verdicts at that count (`exact/direct_effect`, `exact/mediated_effect`) survive the floor. But **eight further `excludes_zero: true` separation verdicts in the same artefact rest on three to six heads** and are withdrawn: ProGen2-medium's exact direct effect, mediated effect and mediated fraction (n = 6, 6, 6), its approximate mediated effect and mediated fraction (n = 4, 3), and the matched pair's *approximate* direct effect, mediated effect and mediated fraction (n = 6, 6, 5). Measured coverage of a nominal 95% interval at these counts is 0.74–0.86, not 0.95 — a simulation recorded in `statistics.py`'s own docstring and not, as yet, in any artefact or test. On re-run those rows publish a point difference and no separation verdict. `results/transfer_20260728/path_patching/panel_summary.json` is retained unmodified as the record.

And it has now been **run**, on all four TG arms, under a seeded permutation of the whole corpus consumed in seeded record order, with the held-out unigram estimator. Artefacts: `results/transfer_gap_20260729_corrected/tg01/`.
>
> | arm | unigram (nats) | clean NLL | gain (bits/symbol) | top-1 | information range (nats) | long-range fraction beyond 8 |
> |---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
> | gpt2-large | 7.587 | 2.632 | 1.615 | 0.472 | 4.884 | 0.254 |
> | protgpt2 | 8.821 | 4.846 | 2.012 | 0.266 | 3.916 | 0.630 |
> | progen2-medium | 2.894 | 1.459 | 2.075 | 0.545 | 1.427 | 0.945 |

None of the 2026-07-24 TG-01 numbers should ever have been read as a property of the model. ZymCTRL's long-range shares are **refused, not reported**: its information range is 0.202 nats, below the 0.5-nat denominator floor, so the shares are not defined. That refusal is the guard working, and it means ZymCTRL contributes no long-range statistic to this stage.

`tg01_information_budget.py` **could not run at all**: `--seed` was registered twice, so argparse raised at parser construction. Verified directly, and consistent with the corrected results tree, which at the time held `tg00`, `tg03`, `tg07` and `tg09` and **no `tg01/`**. *(That tree now also holds `tg01/`, written by the EXP-R2-068 re-run recorded in the banner above; the sentence describes the state that diagnosed the defect, not the tree today.)*

So TG-01's only outputs sat in the retracted 2026-07-24 tree, produced under the broken ProtGPT2 rendering. Its row in §0.1 should be read as **"not re-run"**, not "corrected". *(§0.1 records its blast radius in prose; it has no table, and the phrase "§0.1's blast-radius table" stood here until 2026-08-10.)* No TG-01 number — unigram 8.872, NLL 7.296, gain 2.636 bits, top-1 0.102, the truncation curve — may be cited until the stage runs.

## 0.1 CLOSED — TG-series rendering contamination repaired; the dissociation is retracted (EXP-R2-062)

**Resolved 2026-07-29.** The defect described below is fixed, its blast radius is measured, and B2 has been run. `tg_common.py` no longer renders anything: it is an adapter over `src/transfer/arms.py`, and every TG cohort is now a seeded permutation of the complete eligible set. Corrected artefacts are under `results/transfer_gap_20260729_corrected/`; the 2026-07-24 tree is retained unmodified because it is cited here; pre-correction sources and a per-number status table are frozen under `/Data2/lzp/bio_archive/legacy/r2_transfer_gap_precorrection_20260729/`.

**B2's binary gate returns NO.** The variance–behaviour dissociation does not reproduce. ProtGPT2's rank-512 loss recovered moves **−0.105 → +0.879** and its alignment gap at rank 512 falls **+1.102 → +0.118**, below ProGen2-medium's +0.326. GPT-2-large is unchanged to three decimals, which is what establishes that the movement is the rendering and not the cohort. On the maximum gap over rank, **ZymCTRL (+0.289) sits 2.4x below the text control (+0.708)**: the decoupling is not modality-conditional. **C4 (reliance-weighted dictionaries, 20–40 GPU-h) loses its stated motivation and must not be started on this basis.**

**A second, independent error in the same result.** The variance-concentration premise was mismeasured for every arm, under either rendering. `PC1` and the participation ratio over all positions are dominated by structurally special tokens. On interior, alphabet-bearing positions: gpt2-large PC1 0.034 / PR 253.4 (its all-position 0.809 / 1.53 is its first token, at 24.8x the interior residual norm); ProtGPT2 0.439 / 4.86 (its 0.971 / 1.06 is the FASTA newline the *correct* rendering introduces); ZymCTRL 0.008 / 577.3; ProGen2-medium 0.520 / 3.69. The recorded contrast "ProtGPT2 0.951 against gpt2-large 0.809" compared two artefacts of different origin. The protein arms do not cluster and the highest effective dimension in the panel is a protein arm. Same hazard class as L6.

**What survives, and is now stronger.** L3 — FVU does not track behavioural fidelity — is demonstrated on clean data: ProtGPT2 has the panel's best FVU by 10x (0.0051) and ranks third of four on loss recovered (0.706), while ZymCTRL has the worst FVU by 50x (0.2525) and ranks second (0.763). Spearman(FVU, loss recovered) = 0.000 corrected, against +0.400 before. TG-09's "~0.35 at every depth" is retracted: ProtGPT2 reads 0.996 / 0.977 / 0.354 / 0.609 / 0.715 across depths, making it the *most* depth-dependent arm rather than a flat one.

**New standing rule (Appendix B).** Report residual-stream spectra on interior, alphabet-bearing positions. A participation ratio over all positions measures the attention sink and the separator tokens.

The original description follows, unchanged, because the reasoning is the record.

---

`scripts/transfer_gap/tg_common.py::protein_input` returned the **plain sequence** for ProtGPT2. Its docstring stated the problem and overrode it:

> "ProtGPT2 was trained on FASTA-like text with newline separators; ZymCTRL and ProGen2 use residue-level vocabularies. **We use the plain sequence for all three** so the compared quantity is next-residue prediction on identical content."

The rendering fix later measured that choice at **1.42 nats/token**, moving ProtGPT2's context information from −1.31 to +2.23 (L11 below). The withdrawal that followed covered some ProtGPT2 rows but **not** TG-03, TG-06, TG-07 or TG-09.

**Consequence.** Results resting on a contaminated ProtGPT2 arm include TG-03 (FVU 0.021, loss recovered 0.336), TG-07 (PC1 0.951, participation ratio 1.1, rank-512 loss recovered −0.105) and TG-09 (~0.35 at every depth). The **variance–behaviour decoupling** result is the load-bearing casualty: it is currently recorded as solid and is the entire motivation for the reliance-weighted dictionary line.

**Action, before anything is built on it:** re-run TG-03 and TG-07 under `fasta_wrapped` on a seeded-permutation cohort (~2 GPU-h). If the dissociation does not reproduce, it is retracted and the method line built on it is dropped. Until then, treat those numbers as provisional. File-order exposure for the same runs is likely minor — the effect converges by n = 200 and these used n = 400.

*Done, EXP-R2-062, 1.8 GPU-h. The dissociation did not reproduce; see the header of this section. The file-order expectation was correct in magnitude and wrong to dismiss: at n = 200 it is worth +0.240 nats/token on ProGen2-medium and −0.079 on ProtGPT2 — small beside the 1.78-nat rendering delta, not zero, and arm-dependent in sign.*

## 1. Headline position, as of today

If the work stopped now, the defensible claims are:

**On part 1 (differences).** ~~One measured difference survives in weakened form: on repeat probes, protein decoders have fewer heads in the *upper tail* of the induction-score distribution than text decoders, after adjusting for a real within-lineage scale effect.~~ **Retired as a panel-level statement on 2026-08-12 (EXP-R2-171); what survives is the designed matched pair and nothing wider.** On the primary repeat probe gpt2-large puts **5.46x** the fraction of heads above the headline prefix-matching cut that ProtGPT2 does, and that contrast is *anti*-confounded rather than merely surviving its confound. The panel-level ordering the sentence above was read off is refuted as a modality contrast, correction-independently, by a byte-level **text** decoder scoring inside the protein range on the same published statistic (§4). It was never a general distributional difference, and its modality increment was always carried by a single protein model. It should not be presented as a modality finding. **Corrected 2026-08-09:** this paragraph read "it **inverts** at the headline threshold on the most defensible probe" and was not updated when EXP-R2-068 landed. That inversion was an artefact of the repeat cohort's file-order draw and does not reproduce: at the headline threshold the ordering **holds** on all three probes (§4, and D1's summary in §9). What replaces the inversion as the precision limit is EXP-R2-073's twelfth arm — the panel-level ordering does not survive its own threshold sweep and, at the cut where it does hold, turns on a single head of ProGen2-small. The defensible statements are the matched-pair ratio (5.46x) ~~and the panel-level pattern~~, not the worst-text-above-best-protein ordering. **Scoped further 2026-08-10 (EXP-R2-155), and the two halves now part company.** A token-space prefix-matching score is diluted by alphabet size: a head matching on single-token identity must spread attention over every earlier copy of that token. Measured per arm on its own tokenizer and corpus, the mean number of earlier same-token positions in 256 tokens is gpt2-large **1.387**, ZymCTRL and ProGen2-medium **8.081**, and ProtGPT2 **0.068**. **The matched-pair ratio is therefore not exposed to this objection — it runs backwards there**, since ProtGPT2's multi-residue BPE makes exact recurrence twenty times rarer than in the text control, so the confound predicts a *higher* protein score and the measurement is 5.46x lower. **The panel-level pattern is exposed**, because the residue-tokenised arms sit at 5.8x the text control's collision rate, the same order as the effect, and in its direction. ~~The panel-level half may not be quoted as a modality contrast until a per-arm collision null is run;~~ **the null was run on 2026-08-12 and the panel-level half does not come back (EXP-R2-171): it is refuted rather than suspended, and permanently retired.** The matched-pair half stands and is reinforced by surviving the objection.

The far-band propagation result, recorded here until 2026-07-31 as the best-founded part-1 result, is **withdrawn as a model claim**: it compared arms at a distance declared in tokens across arms differing 4.4x in symbols per token, and two *protein* arms swap order on that choice alone (§5.1, EXP-R2-073). The strongest surviving part-1 measurement is therefore §5.05(d)'s pathway budget, whose statistic is a within-arm dimensionless ratio and is not exposed to the same hazard — with the two caveats that already travel with it.

**On part 2 (limitations).** This is where the programme's real yield is, and much of it was demonstrated *on the text control*, which makes it a property of the method rather than of protein models. See §4.

> **The current part-2 position in three sentences, because the rest of this section is a stack of banners recording how it was arrived at.** *One:* on copy suppression, a prediction-addressed-attention census fails to recover an arm's own causally important heads on **every residue-tokenised protein decoder measured** — two architectures, three parameter scales, three pretraining corpora, both conditioning states — while **every text decoder recovers well above its own chance level, including a byte-level one whose tokenisation is symbol-level** (§D2.c; EXP-R2-126, EXP-R2-128, EXP-R2-129). Architecture, conditioning, scale, corpus and symbol granularity are each excluded by a control built to exclude it, so the failure is a property of the protein arms and not of their tokenizer — and the same byte-level control shows that granularity *does* collapse the all-grid rank correlation, which is why that correlation is not the statistic carrying any of this. *Two:* on induction, the analogous separation is **depth-confounded on every arm including the text controls**, so L22 is a limitation of the method rather than of the transfer (EXP-R2-120, converged at K=10 by EXP-R2-123). *Three:* neither of D2.c's rank-correlation separations survives the cross-lab arms — each gap is smaller than one boundary arm's own spread across draws — so the retrieval statistic, measured against each arm's own chance level, is the only one carrying a claim. Everything below is the record of how those three were reached, including the readings that were withdrawn on the way.

> **The replacement-fidelity line, added 2026-08-09 and stated separately because it is a different estimand from the census result above.** Under sequential per-layer transcoder replacement at a fixed 12x expansion, behavioural recovery separates the modalities by a factor of six to ten with no overlap: text **+0.9084 to +0.9322** across two decoders, protein **+0.0916 to +0.1641** across three. Method, dictionary training budget, depth and sparse routing were each excluded by a control built to exclude it (EXP-R2-141 to 147), and **tokenisation was excluded by EXP-R2-149**, which admitted ZymCTRL — gpt2 architecture, 36 x 1280, protein, one token per residue — and measured **+0.0916 and +0.0923** on two corpus seeds against the shape-identical text control's +0.9322. The protein side now spans three tokenisations and two architectures and fails on all of them. Two qualifications travel with it. ZymCTRL's own dictionary reconstructs about twice as badly as either comparator (0.529 per layer against 0.275 and 0.238), so for that arm alone "the dictionary was worse" is not excluded and a higher-budget control is running; and ZymCTRL differs from ProtGPT2 in tokenisation **and** in EC conditioning, so the result separates the modality reading from "tokenisation or conditioning" rather than from tokenisation alone. **The causal half of this gate is not part of the claim** — EXP-R2-148 withdrew it, because the text control that recovers 93% behaviourally fails the causal-rank gate itself on the identical 720-head grid (L27). What this line does **not** do is explain why; the free linear baseline was the standing clue and EXP-R2-149 weakened it, since ZymCTRL's free baseline sits at zero rather than with the other protein arms, so that statistic's apparent two-group structure is a fact about EXP-R2-144's four arms and not a modality statement.

**On part 3 (methods for biological knowledge and discovery).** No proposed method has yet passed causal, retrieval-aware, and independent biological validation, and no new biological knowledge has been established. D3.g and D3.h are gated candidates tied to explicit knowledge questions and measured interface or translation problems; D3.e and D3.f remain closed, and **D3.b closed on 2026-08-12 with its motivating premise refuted on the arms it was written about** (EXP-R2-173, EXP-R2-178; §9's D3 table carries the closure). That closure is a *measured* negative and not an instrument failure — the positive control cleared on every arm-concept cell — and it left a method limitation behind it, L28, which is directly exposed on an existing lens readout. **D3.d was re-chartered to run independent of D3.c and halted at Stage 1 the same day**, on the refusal its own pre-registration named: the corpus-coupling baseline that its second-order subtraction depends on fails its own positive control on every protein it could be estimated for, so the design cannot clear the bar it was admitted for — established at zero GPU cost. Its attainability gate is separately informative rather than merely failed, because the instrument is validated on the text control, and §9's D3.d row carries both. **The retrieval-aware leg of that bar did move the same day, and it moved on the evaluation side rather than the method side:** a corpus-disjoint referent now exists and is certified, so a protein decoder's phenotype prediction has for the first time been measured with retrieval excluded by construction instead of estimated and subtracted — and the first measurement through it is negative in an unanticipated way, the model surviving the corpus fragment baselines and losing to a one-parameter hydrophobicity scale (F12, and D1.e for the certificate). **That last clause is the one most likely to be quoted and the one most likely to be misread, so it does not travel alone**: the loss is confirmed to be a property of *designed* sequence rather than of short sequence, and on its own specificity control it is a broad degradation of this decoder's ranking there rather than a hydropathy-specific defeat — six of seven baselines carry the same interaction. F12 carries both qualifications and the composition bound that limits them. That is an evaluation instrument this programme did not have, not a method that has passed validation, and no biological knowledge follows from it. A candidate is a testable direction, not a result; a candidate that refuses itself early is the discipline working.

### 1.1 Frozen claims register, 2026-08-06

The foundational programme is closed as of this date (§9.1). This register exists so that the next question asked of this repository is a *new* one: everything below is measured, its artefacts are named, and **re-measuring any of it is explicitly not authorised**. The citable set is this register together with the post-freeze additions in §1.2; a claim absent from both is not citable, whatever it says elsewhere in this document. The surrounding sections keep the reasoning, including readings that were withdrawn, because how a result was reached is part of the record.

**What the register governs, clarified 2026-08-10.** It governs **claims** — statements this programme asserts about models or methods, of the form "as it may be stated" in the middle column. It does not void the **measurements** narrated elsewhere: the corrected TG-01 table, §5.1's quantitative anchors, L9's channel figures, D2.c's per-arm values and EXP-R2-074's decay ratios remain quotable *as measurements*, each with the artefacts, cohort band and bounds recorded beside it. The rule that binds is the promotion step: a measurement may not be restated as a claim about models or methods without entering the register, and until it does, it is quoted with its bounds and not as a finding. The sentence above read simply "A claim absent from this register … is not citable" and, taken at face value, made almost every number in this document non-citable while the surrounding prose presented those numbers as standing — a boundary the document had never drawn.

| # | Claim, as it may be stated | Evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| F1 | A prediction-addressed-attention census on a **residue-tokenised protein decoder** does not recover that decoder's own causally important heads above its own chance level; every text decoder measured clears 3.6×. **Sharpened by EXP-R2-131**: on ProGen2-small the census is not merely at chance but **below a selector that knows only a head's layer index** (1 hit against 4), so it is anti-informative relative to the head's own coordinates | `results/transfer_20260801/d2c_panel_dfix_draw*/`, `d2c_protgpt2_dfix_*`, `d2c_textctl_dfix_*`, `d2c_zymctrl_draw*`; ~55 arm-draws at one condition; read with `scripts/transfer/read_paa_panel.py` | **frozen, sharpened** |
| F1b | **ProtGPT2's pass is substantially depth-carried and is not evidence that its census transfers.** Its within-layer partial ρ is negative on every draw (−0.2286 … −0.0682), and a trivial depth-only selector recovers 5 of the 6 heads its census does, where on gpt2-large the same baseline recovers 0 against the census's 7. What survives is that the arm sits above its own chance level; what does not is the inference that the *census* is doing the work | EXP-R2-131, reproduced independently from `causal.json` over the on-condition draws | **frozen; re-derived 2026-08-06 under a repaired seeding, unchanged** |
| F1b(i) | **Two additions from that re-derivation, and one repair that had to precede it.** The audit seeded each draw by its **position in the discovery list**, so pulling eight ByGPT5 draws to B shifted every later draw's tie-break jitter and moved the depth-only figure on 8 of 62 otherwise identical arm-draws — while the deterministic census figure stayed bit-identical. Seeding is now derived from the draw's directory, verified independent of what else is in the run. Under it, F1b's two quoted comparisons are **unchanged** (gpt2-large 7 against 0; ProGen2-small 1 against 4). *New:* the byte-level text control reads **census 11 against depth-only 1**, so the trivial baseline is weak on a symbol-level *text* arm too, which removes the last reading in which the baseline's strength could be a tokenisation artefact. *Also new, and stated as a caution rather than a claim:* pooled over all six on-condition ProtGPT2 draws rather than the four F1b quotes, its census median falls to 3.5 while depth-only holds at 5.5 — the direction F1b already asserts, but its **point value remains non-citable** (§1.1's own note) and no reversal is claimed on it | `logs/paa_audit_seedfix/`, 62 on-condition arm-draws | **frozen** |
| F2 | That failure is not explained by architecture, conditioning, scale, pretraining corpus, or symbol-level tokenisation — each excluded by a control built to exclude it, the last being a byte-level **text** decoder at 5.3× against ProGen2-small's 0.5× on an identical 192-head grid | EXP-R2-128 (ZymCTRL, K=4), EXP-R2-129 (ByGPT5-medium, K=4); `bygpt5_cohort_power_20260806` on GPFS qualifies the control arm at +2.462 nats | **frozen** |
| F3 | **No proximal cause exists at the instance level, and the "causal target is noise" account is falsified rather than merely unsupported.** Best held-out stratification gain is +1 hit against a negative control ranging −3 to +1; and both rankings reproduce across independent draws on every failing arm — causal top-20 at 5.3–21.6× chance, census top-20 at 9.1–36×. They are two stable rankings that disagree. ProtGPT2 has the panel's *lowest* per-head SNR and passes; ZymCTRL has higher SNR on the same grid and reads chance | EXP-R2-131; `scripts/transfer/paa_failure_audit.py`, 62 on-condition arm-draws, pre-registered factors and discovery/held-out split | **closed — FAIL, as pre-registered** |
| F4 | An all-grid rank correlation between a census score and a query-local causal readout does not measure census validity: it is confounded with layer depth on every arm (EXP-R2-120) **and** with tokenisation granularity (EXP-R2-129, where the byte arm is protein-like on the correlation and text-like on retrieval) | `results/transfer_20260801/d2bprime_multidraw/`, and the D2.c panel roots | **frozen** |
| F5 | On induction, the analogous census-to-causal separation is depth-confounded on every arm including the text controls, so L22 is a limitation of the **method** rather than of the transfer. Converged at K=10 on both boundary arms; further draws not authorised | EXP-R2-115/120/122/123 | **frozen** |
| F6 | The tuned lens improves on the logit lens on every scored arm at both bands, but **the size of that advantage on protein is a function of a cohort band chosen for compute**: the text control gains 4% on the qualifying band while every protein arm loses 54–82% | `results/lens_origband_20260805/`, `results/lens_qualband2_20260805/` | **frozen** |
| F7 | The MLP-to-attention pathway budget separates the modalities with non-overlapping ranges, reproduced to two decimals on corpus-wide seeded cohorts | `results/transfer_20260730/`, §5.05(d) | **frozen**, with its two standing caveats |
| F8 | The **thirty-two** catalogued limitations of §5 (L1–L32; recorded here as twenty-three until 2026-08-09, then twenty-six before L27 was added the same day, twenty-seven until 2026-08-12, when L28 to L31 were added, and thirty-one until 2026-08-14, when L32 was added), each scoped as **method** or **transfer** under §5's own organising rule — no row carries any other scope | as cited per row in §5 | **frozen as a rule; the count moves with the catalogue** |
| F9 | A protein arm's cohort carries a selection uncertainty its matched text control does not, on seven unrelated statistics | §5.05(b), EXP-R2-069/070(iv)/077/121/127, and the D2.c retrieval spread | **frozen as a pattern**, not as a single measurement |

### 1.2 Post-freeze citable additions, 2026-08-10

| # | Claim, as it may be stated | Evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| F10 | On 217 ProteinGym substitution assays grouped into 174 wild-type families, none of the three measured protein decoders exceeds a position-independent profile lookup over the available pretraining-corpus evidence. ProtGPT2, the only arm with an exactly identified staged corpus, has MODEL − LOOKUP **−0.0143 [−0.0381, +0.0101]** and is retrieval-bounded. ProGen2-medium uses a lower-bound corpus proxy and reads **−0.0043 [−0.0303, +0.0216]**, also retrieval-bounded. ProGen3-112M uses the most model-favouring lower-bound proxy and reads **−0.0808 [−0.1139, −0.0481]**, retrieval-dominated. The last category was added after seeing the ProGen3 result and is disclosed as such. This bounds the present fitness interface; it does not show memorisation, absence of biological knowledge, or lack of generative novelty, and it does not extend to joint models | EXP-R2-143; `results/transfer/retrieval_bound/` | **citable with corpus-proxy and interface bounds** |
| F11 | Under sequential per-layer transcoder replacement at fixed 12× expansion, two text decoders recover **0.9084–0.9322** of their clean-to-ablated behavioural gap, while three protein decoders recover **0.0916–0.1641** on their declared evaluation cohorts. The protein comparison is restricted to the shared 64–246-residue band. Controls exclude the method being wholly invalid and exclude depth, basic training budget, sparse routing, and multi-residue BPE as sufficient explanations; they do not identify the proximal cause. The causal-rank statistic is withdrawn because its high-recovery text control also fails it. ZymCTRL's EC conditioning remains a permanent limitation, because the control that would split it from tokenisation is not buildable under this estimand at any budget. **Its dictionary-quality objection is closed (EXP-R2-153, 2026-08-10):** retraining the same arm's dictionary from 22,000 to 28,000 steps — **~154 tokens per latent, above the passing text control's 132** — moved reconstruction by 1.8% and behavioural recovery from **+0.0916 to +0.0932**, overlapping intervals. The dictionary is saturated, so "it was simply under-trained" is not available, and this arm's contribution to the three-arm statement is at full strength. **Band-robustness added 2026-08-10 (EXP-R2-150):** swept over four cohort bands from 64 to ~1022 residues, three arms and four dictionaries, the protein side spans **0.0425–0.2724** against text at 0.9084–0.9322 — worst case a factor of **3.3 with no overlap** — so the separation survives the most adverse band available. Absolute per-arm numbers must still be quoted with their band, because three of four dictionaries are band-dependent and **not in a shared direction** (ProGen3 falls, ProtGPT2 rises, ZymCTRL is flat at 1.23×). **They must be quoted with their expansion factor for the same reason (EXP-R2-197):** swept at 2×, 4×, 8× and 12× on `gpt2` with everything else held, recovery moves monotonically from **+0.8358 to +0.9088**, so the 12× in this claim is a condition of its numbers and not an immaterial setting.
