| L26 | **A replacement-model recovery ratio had no free baseline for as long as it was measured, and when one was finally supplied the method cleared it decisively.** `15_replacement_faithfulness.py` scored the original, the method, and the mean-ablated floor that is the *denominator* — nothing stood between the floor and the method, so standing rule 28 was unmet at this stage from the day it was written, across EXP-R2-132, 135 and 138. Supplied (EXP-R2-139) as one affine map per block solved in closed form on a disjoint cohort, 1,478,400 parameters against a CLT's 115,065,600: it recovers **−1.61 to −1.64**, far worse than the mean-ablation floor it is compared against, and **saturated** — a sixteenfold increase in fitting data moves it by 0.003, so the failure is about how non-linear the blocks are and not about the fit. Every transcoder measured, ours and the released one, beats it. The limitation is therefore about the *stage*, not about the method it was applied to: a recovery ratio quoted for years without a free baseline could not have distinguished a dictionary that captured something from a number any smooth map would produce, and it happens that this one can. Rule 28's other consequence stands: "better than free" and "good enough to carry a causal claim" are different questions, and the arms still fail the second | SAE / transcoder / CLT, any recovery-ratio design | EXP-R2-139 | **method** — a missing control in the evaluation, found on our own instrument |
| L25 | **AND THE ORDERING IT RESOLVES DOES NOT SURVIVE INTO FAITHFULNESS (EXP-R2-138, corrected 2026-08-07).** Put through the replacement gate at band 64–246, the parameter-matched PLT's clear reconstruction advantage over the CLT becomes **no resolved advantage**: NLL recovery 0.1450 against 0.1464, with the two seed-paired differences **opposite in sign** (−0.0111, +0.0139) and the design's minimum detectable difference (0.0546 on 2 df) exceeding the entire between-arm range (0.0436), so non-separation is a bound on resolution rather than a measured null — the phrase here was "inside a seed spread sixty times the difference", in which 0.0004 was the CLT's own seed range and not the between-arm difference of 0.001404; attention-head rank agreement 0.4999 against 0.4884; MoE-block agreement 0.739 against 0.715 against per-arm spreads of 0.18 and 0.13. All seven arms — including the released PLT, which reproduces EXP-R2-132's 0.127 and 0.489 exactly and so validates the comparison — are PARTIAL, with behavioural recovery of 0.11–0.16 against an 0.80 gate and no arm's attention ρ clearing 0.5 on its lower bound, while attainability passes throughout at ceilings of 0.97–0.99. **This is L3's shape arriving on the CLT-versus-PLT question**, and sharper than L3's original form: the architectures are separable on reconstruction and not separable on either property a circuit claim needs. The defensible summary is that at this budget the cross-layer/per-layer choice is not what determines whether a replacement can carry a causal claim, because none of them can. **MEASURED, AND IT RESOLVES AGAINST THE ARCHITECTURE (EXP-R2-136, 2026-08-07).** At equal dictionary width the CLT wins by 0.288 in held-out NMSE sum (2.184 against 2.472, 11.7%) — ProGenMech's comparison, reproducing in direction under a protocol where the arms share data, order, seed, step budget and optimiser. **At equal parameters it loses by 0.126**: a PLT widened to `d_hidden` 14963 reaches 2.058. Paired within corpus seed — the arms share a seed and a bit-identical token budget — the differences are +0.1294 and +0.1228, 95% CI [0.084, 0.168]; the ordering holds at all ten evaluation checkpoints in both seeds and reproduces on an independent Swiss-Prot cohort at 3.919 against 4.221. The wide PLT is better at **eight** of ten layers, the CLT winning layers 8 and 9, which are the two with the most cross-layer sources writing into them. So on this objective the CLT's advantage is **capacity, not cross-layer connectivity — under a parameter or wall-clock budget, and not under an inference-FLOP budget**, where the matched arm's 3.0x active parameters per token reverse it. Two scope limits travel with it: the equal-width arms are nested, since zeroing the off-diagonal decoders turns the CLT into the PLT exactly; and the campaign ran `auxk = 128` rather than the declared 192, because the entry point's default shadowed the config. Three bounds travel with it: it is *reconstruction*, not faithfulness, which is the distinction EXP-R2-132/135 exist to enforce; the matched arm is sparser in relative terms (k=64 is 1.39% of 4608 and 0.43% of 14963, and no PLT matches both); and the arms are matched on data and parameters but not on wall clock (4.33 h against 3.46 h at identical steps and tokens). The row as originally written follows. **A cross-layer-versus-per-layer transcoder comparison at equal dictionary width is also a comparison at 3.25x the parameters, and the released configuration does not separate them.** A CLT over ten layers carries `L(L+1)/2 = 55` decoders against a PLT's 10, so at `d_hidden` 4608 it holds **115,065,600** parameters against **35,439,360** and takes 1.78x the wall clock. ProGenMech's own training scripts set both arms to `D_HIDDEN=4608`, so their headline claim that cross-layer connectivity wins is confounded with capacity in exactly the way standing rule 28 names: the baseline available from the design's own coordinates — a PLT wide enough to match the parameter count, `d_hidden` 14963 here — was never run. The confound is not removable by any single control, because no PLT can match a CLT on parameters *and* on the active fraction of its dictionary at fixed `k`: 64 latents are 1.39% of 4608 and 0.43% of 14963. What a matched arm buys is the ability to say which of the two accounts survives, not to eliminate the choice | cross-layer transcoders, any capacity-asymmetric architecture comparison | EXP-R2-136; ProGenMech's released `run_clt.py` / `run_plt.py` | **method** — present in the external released configuration, and reproduced here before it was noticed |

Behavioural and causal-rank fidelity come apart, and it is the behavioural gate alone that carries EXP-R2-147's modality attribution. *Recorded descriptively and not as a claim:* top-10 overlap on the matched 720-head grid is 4 for gpt2-large against 0 for ProtGPT2 (expected 0.139), but ProtGPT2 has the panel's lowest ceiling (0.6949) and its original resolves only **153 of 720** heads above zero effect against gpt2-large's **480**, so most of its grid is ties and its correlation is attenuated before any modality effect is reached | sequential replacement, any rank-agreement gate over per-component ablation effects | EXP-R2-148 | **method** — the third estimand in this programme whose apparent modality separation did not survive its own text control, with L22 and F5 |
| L28 | **A coarsened lens readout on a small alphabet is uninterpretable without a matched null, and the correction is large.** Every coarsening of a distribution resolves earlier than the distribution it coarsens — collapsing twenty residues onto four classes takes the 64–246 band's residue marginal from 4.1566 bits to about 1.58 — so "this decoder resolves chemical class before residue identity" carries no information on its own. Measured against a shuffled-property null, the null is **centred near zero** (means −0.008 to +0.017 across arms) and its **upper tail is heavy**: the 0.999 quantile reaches **+0.46 to +0.64** on a twenty-symbol alphabet against **+0.16 to +0.30** on 50,257. The mechanism is the part worth remembering — with only twenty values to permute, the best of a thousand shuffles is nearly as good as the truth. ProtGPT2's final-layer hydropathy readout is **+0.667**, which is exactly the figure a concept-lens result would be built on; against the 0.999 quantile of its own null (+0.526) the margin is **+0.14** and it clears at 3 of 11 depths, while the text arms observe +0.68 to +0.83 against quantiles of +0.16 to +0.30. The same instrument is about four times more discriminating on 50,257 symbols than on 20. **A coarsened readout on a small alphabet without a matched null is not weak evidence; it is uninterpretable.** *Directly exposed:* `lenses.residue_class_trajectory` reads every protein arm's lens distribution through one hand-declared four-way chemical partition and reports whether the coarse term resolves at shallower depth than the fine one, with no null of any kind | logit lens, tuned lens, DLA, any coarsened or property-projected readout | EXP-R2-173, EXP-R2-178 | **method** — the aperture gain the design was built to exploit is present and **largest on gpt2-large** (+0.195 at τ=0.50), where no aperture problem was ever claimed and the vocabulary is 50,257, so it is a property of coarsening rather than of a low-rank protein interface |
