Two construction repairs preceded it, both made before any campaign and both argued from mechanism rather than from a measured value: a shared substituting symbol gave the control pairs half the effect they existed to exclude (2B against B), and a one-symbol repeat is not a dependency any arm is claimed to have learned — so the geometry was repaired under rule 2 rather than the verdict recorded. *The two protein failures are not the same failure.* ProGen2-medium's interval tightened from [−0.13, +0.27] at 24 probes to **[−0.025, +0.100]** at 128, its point estimate sits *inside* its own null (q99 +0.068), and its top-1 rate is 0.172 against 0.125 chance — with **every** planted repeat surviving tokenisation, so no interface excuse is available to it. ProtGPT2's carries no attribution at all, because only 21% of its planted repeats survive its tokenizer, which is L31. **Whether ProGen2-medium's null reflects an absent mechanism or a probe off-distribution for proteins is open: one geometry was tested, no distribution control was run, and the analogue of the control that answers this for the induction census does not exist here.** The re-charter and the design as written follow, for the record. **RE-CHARTERED 2026-08-12: it ran independent of D3.c**, because its causal object is a sequence *position* rather than a dictionary feature — so no surviving feature set is a prerequisite, and no pair dictionary is trained. The estimand is **measured pairwise epistasis**: per position pair carrying at least ten measured doubles whose both constituent singles are also measured, the pair-mean residual of the observed double-mutant phenotype about an isotonic regression on the additive prediction from the two singles, correlated against the model's own predicted pairwise interaction, with the wild-type protein clustered at 50% identity as the unit — F10's unit. **F10 does not already answer it, and that is algebra rather than conjecture:** both channels that bound F10 are exact first-order sums, so both predict identically zero epistasis for every multi-substitution variant, and a pairwise estimand lies outside their expressive class. **No text analogue of a DMS assay exists**, so standing rule 2 is met in the only available form — a **synthetic planted-coupling referent**, where the coupling is known by construction and which *can* be run on the text control; A1 passes only if the bootstrap interval clears the within-probe permutation null on gpt2-large **and** on at least one protein arm, and an A1 failure means the instrument cannot detect a coupling planted for it, which stops Stage 2 and is reported as a method failure rather than as a null about models. **Stage 0 (CPU) and Stage 1 (A1) are authorised and pre-registered before any model was loaded; nothing beyond them is.** Two measured constraints reshape it rather than qualify it: about three quarters of raw pair-level epistasis is the assay's own global nonlinearity — removing the isotonic transform costs 55–77% of the pair-level spread on six of ten assays — and the split-half reliability of what survives is 0.42–0.99, so every correlation is quoted beside an attenuation ceiling; and ProtGPT2 leaves exactly one token differing on only **47–55%** of single substitutions, with the retained half being the BPE-stable half rather than a random one, so it enters the behavioural half at full strength and the internal-mediation half only with a measured retained-versus-dropped comparison. Its Phase A is also the powered relational attempt §7's second standing rejection names, and it did not discharge that gate. The phenotype, clinical and population evidence staged for it is held outside the repository; A.3 records where | Stage 0 on CPU and Stage 1 on one GPU, both spent; Stage 2's 10–20 GPU-h never authorised and now not owed |

The Phase B items above have prior claim to `B1` and `B2` here, so this document writes the D3.h criteria as **D3.h-A**, **D3.h-B1** and **D3.h-B2** throughout, and a log citation of "Criterion B1" or "Criterion B2" in the EXP-R2-191 series resolves to those rather than to the rows above. This is the hazard Appendix A.4 records for experiment ids, reaching item names instead.

### 9.1 Abandoned, with reasons

**Terminated 2026-08-06, as a register rather than a habit.** Each of these is
work that can still be *done* and can no longer *change a conclusion*, which is
the only test that matters once an instrument is characterised. Nothing below is
reopened without a claim that depends on it.

- **Further draws of the vanilla PAA or induction census.** D2.c is closed at ~55
  arm-draws; the classification has been stable since K=4 on every arm and the
  rank correlations carry nothing. The last queue was stopped mid-flight on
  2026-08-06 and the four lanes then running were allowed to finish because
  killing them would have wasted spent compute and left partial artefacts.
- **Further vanilla SAE, CLT or transcoder variants at new widths or seeds.** The
  existing dictionary work is scaled enough; what it lacks is causal-faithfulness
  validation and biological grounding, which more of the same does not supply.
- **Re-running TG stages for file completeness** (see D2.d).
- **Retired EC steering, atlas, drug-design and venue-specific lines** — already
  frozen at `/Data2/lzp/bio_archive/legacy/r2_retired_scope_20260729/`.
- **Property-conditioned J-Lens / J-Space** — standing rejection, §7.
- **Residue-pair transcoders and reliance-weighted dictionaries** — motivation
  retracted (§7; C4 dropped when B2 returned NO).
- **A natural-language autoencoder as the core protein thread** — bounded by L9's
  closure argument before it starts.
- **Unpaired token-level text-versus-protein feature crosscoders.** A model-diffing Crosscoder compares checkpoints on the same inputs, so unrelated text and protein token positions do not define a target. D3.h admits only identical-input checkpoint comparisons. D3.g uses genuine sequence–description records after sequence-level or declared concept-level aggregation and does not treat raw hidden features as inherently identical across modalities.
- **Attribution graphs without ground truth.** An attribution graph over protein features with no DMS, structural or functional referent cannot be validated, and L7 already prices the frozen-attention approximation it rests on. D3.i does not reopen this design: it requires an independently validated protein circuit before translation and external biological evidence afterward.
- **More attention heat maps, UMAP projections and probe-accuracy tables.** L16
  settled that decodability is not reliance; another accuracy figure adds nothing.

1. **Expansion of the present `paa_specific` screen to further mechanisms** — closed by its GPT-2-large copy-suppression control (L5). A different screen or an exhaustive causal effect-size census requires a new, independently gated design.
2. **Normalised MLP/attention share as a modality instrument** — moved four times on a quantity of magnitude ~0.4. More seeds will not fix a fragile denominator. Keep the unnormalised per-scope ΔCE values.
3. **J-Lens as a fitted cross-arm coefficient** — keep the algebraic rank, drop the regression.
4. **Pair / residue-pair transcoders** — standing rejection, motivation retracted at ~0.03 AUC over control.
5. **Property-conditioned Jacobian in its probe-derived form** — standing rejection. D3.b does not revive it: the Concept-aligned Lens candidate uses a pre-declared token-to-property map for within-protein projections, not a probe-derived hidden-space direction, and permits cross-modality claims only on modality-neutral abstractions. It remained descriptive until rank-matched controls and causal intervention passed, and **D3.b has since closed on those very controls (2026-08-12): two of its three declared properties clear the rank-matched partition null at zero of eleven depths on every protein arm, so the causal-intervention half was never reached.** The standing rejection is unaffected either way — it was never what closed D3.b. *(Cited as "§8.3" until 2026-08-09; §8's items have never been numbered as subsections, and item 3 is the one the retired-name table maps to C2 → D3.b.)*
6. **The 2x2 training campaign** — the most expensive design available and it cannot fix the confound that matters: matching architecture, parameters, scale and budget does not match the data-generating process. Report as an irreducible limit.
7. **Searching for a second subword protein LM** — structurally impossible with current public models.
8. **Steering re-runs before a dense readout exists** — without one, a steering run is not a test.
9. **Further investment in the induction modality claim.**

### 9.2 ~~One unresolved question worth 5 GPU-hours~~ — answered (EXP-R2-068)

- ~~**Does non-local propagation survive at production scale?**~~ **Run at 1.5 GPU-h. It survives as an ordering and fails as a modality claim.** Every arm clears the case gate and every interval excludes zero, but the matched pair — the only modality-identifying comparison the panel has — overlaps at [0.202, 0.215]. What separates is ProGen2-medium from both other arms, which is an architecture and tokenisation contrast, and ZymCTRL cannot enter the estimand at all because its conditioning prompt puts the scored span outside the patching window. The strongest available substrate claim is therefore **not** a modality claim. Detail in §5.1.

- **Follow-on, and it narrowed further (EXP-R2-069), then resolved (EXP-R2-070), then was withdrawn (EXP-R2-073 — see §5.1).** A disjoint second window removed ProtGPT2 from the ordering entirely, leaving ProGen2-medium as the only arm above the text control in both draws at every threshold, so the surviving statement was about one lineage rather than one modality. The five-window equal-case successor confirmed that narrowing and confined the separation to the large-effect tail. **All of it is now withdrawn as a model claim**, because every one of those comparisons was made at a band declared in tokens across arms that differ 4.4x in symbols per token, and the ordering between the two protein arms reverses under a content-symbol band. The lineage reading is withdrawn along with the modality reading; what remains is a well-executed measurement of an estimand that was not identified.

## Appendix A — Experiment index

**Scope, declared 2026-08-09: this index covers the founding series only — TR-025 through EXP-R2-066 — and is not carried forward.** It was written when that series was the whole record and its preamble read as though it were the register of the experimental record. **`docs/EXPERIMENT_LOG.md` is the chronological register for the whole programme, and §1 is the authority on what is currently citable.** Extending A.1 and A.2 would build a second full index of the log and a third source of claim status. They are kept, unextended, because their status column carries retraction records — TR-030 retracted by EXP-R2-033, both EXP-R2-050 rows, TG-01 — that exist nowhere else in this document, and because A.4's provenance hazard depends on their aliases.

Within that scope, "stands" means the result was defensible when this index was written; "retracted" means withdrawn on evidence; "superseded" means replaced by a better-controlled measurement. Current status for anything cited outside this appendix is §1's, not this column's.

**Stable aliases for reused transfer-series ids.** The append-only log reused EXP-R2-025 through EXP-R2-032. This audit therefore uses `TR-025` = EXP-R2-025 (2026-07-24), `TR-026` = EXP-R2-026 (2026-07-27), and `TR-027` through `TR-032` = EXP-R2-027 through EXP-R2-032 (2026-07-28). These aliases do not renumber or rewrite history.

### A.3 Infrastructure and provenance

EXP-R2-035 (results-wipe diagnosis), EXP-R2-037 (H200 migration), EXP-R2-041/042/043/044 (controller/worker refactor, contract reconciliation, port validation, dispatch split), EXP-R2-049 (cohort ceiling), EXP-R2-051/052 (staging; code-freeze scope), EXP-R2-058 (GPFS staging and load checks for all eleven checkpoints; full SHA-256 for ten, byte-size-only evidence for Qwen2.5-0.5B), EXP-R2-063 (`scripts/transfer/` audit: one panel contract replaces five hand-maintained arm lists; cohort-band and panel qualifications), EXP-R2-065 (documents, logs and results reorganised against this document's objective), EXP-R2-066 (live import closure, scoring/TG contracts, TG-01 non-runnability and corrected-stage status).

**Where the staged biological evidence went.** The external phenotype, clinical and population trees staged for D3.d — gnomAD, ClinVar, BioLiP, IndelMissense, PhosphoSitePlus and an empty COSMIC placeholder — were moved off the working filesystem on 2026-08-12 under storage pressure and now live at `/Data2/lzp/bio_archive/interpretability_transfer_offload_20260812/`, which is their authoritative location and carries its own README and a re-checkable `SHA256SUMS` over every file. Nothing was deleted, and their absence from every live code path is what staging ahead of a gated item looks like rather than evidence of abandonment. The same archive holds the April ProtGPT2 CLT run's optimizer state and the model-weight payloads smoke checks had left under `logs/smoke/` (Appendix B rule 29). `docs/PROJECT_LOG.md` records the move and its integrity evidence.

**Where the retired scope went.** The conserved sparse-readout atlas, EC steering, enzyme design, the npj manuscript package, the P0 protocol set, the npj literature corpus and the recoverability preregistration are at `/Data2/lzp/bio_archive/legacy/r2_retired_scope_20260729/`. Four things were kept live because this programme depends on them: `results/final_checkpoints/` (the only protein dictionaries held locally, an input to C1), `results/transfer_gap_20260724/` (cited in §0.1), the `evidence/p0_2*` receipt chain (the evidence behind L1 and the baseline C1 re-qualifies), and the April CLT training logs. Note that the 27 P0-2 dictionaries are **not** in `results/final_checkpoints/` — they are on GPFS, and their paths and SHA-256 digests are in `evidence/p0_2b_fidelity_20260727/p0_2b_fidelity_spec.executed.json`.

### A.4 Provenance hazard

**Experiment ids EXP-R2-025 through EXP-R2-032 are each used twice** in `EXPERIMENT_LOG.md` — once by the July 17–27 npj-revision series and once by the July 24–28 transfer series. Historical entries remain unchanged. Current transfer-series citations must use the `TR-025` through `TR-032` aliases defined above or include both date and original id. Ids from EXP-R2-033 onward are unique.

---

## Appendix B — Methodological hazards, as standing rules

Each earned by a failure in this programme.

1. **Never take the first N records of a biological corpus.** Sample under a seeded permutation and report a skip-offset sensitivity. (Three instances.)
2. **Check gate attainability on the text control before applying it to a protein arm.** A gate the positive control cannot pass is a specification defect. (Six instances: the two this rule was written on, a third recorded at §D2.c's blocker list, a fourth at §8 item 0b, and both adequacy criteria of the D3.h diffing unit, voided on their own text control on 2026-08-13 and 2026-08-14. Recorded as two until 2026-08-09 and as four until 2026-08-14.) **Attainability is a property of the statistic as much as of the control, and one of those six failed on arithmetic before any control was read**: a threshold set at the ambient dimension is unreachable by an `r99`, which cannot exceed `ceil(0.99 d)`, so the branch keyed to it was decided before the measurement ran. Check that the statistic can reach the number, then that the control does. (L32.) **First evidence the rule is being applied ahead of a run rather than only recorded after one, 2026-08-14:** the text control arm added to EXP-R2-204 was checked for its own ceiling before the cards were spent — `base/text` is already 92.87% live, so its live-fraction ratio cannot exceed **1.077** whatever the data does — and the arm's primary reference was reassigned to `stage1/text` at 59.82% live, with headroom to 1.672, while the ceiling cell was kept deliberately for the one thing it can still show.
3. **Use held-out, never plug-in, estimators for information decompositions.**
4. **Feed every arm the format it was trained on**, and verify the rendering against the model's own likelihood.
5. **An intervention that moves everything needs a control for moving everything.**
6. **A reconstruction gate does not protect against wrong norm algebra** — RMSNorm-on-LayerNorm passes at 0.49% error while corrupting attribution.
7. **Verify that environment defaults have not narrowed the panel**; print resolved paths before every campaign.
8. **Distribution-body statistics require decoy/positional correction; tail statistics do not.**
9. **Never run `git clean` under this repository** — it is its own git repository with `/results/` ignored. This destroyed all experiment results three times.
10. **Search the literature by mechanism name, not by domain**, before designing a track. (Cost: an entire induction track designed without finding prior work that had already established induction heads in protein LMs.)
11. **Compute residual-stream spectra on interior, alphabet-bearing positions.** A participation ratio or top-PC share taken over all positions measures the attention sink and the format separators, not the representation. GPT-2-large reads PC1 0.809 over all positions and **0.034** with its first token dropped (participation ratio 1.53 → 253.4); ProtGPT2 reads 0.971 and **0.439** with its FASTA newlines dropped (1.06 → 4.86). Two headline numbers that were compared to each other turned out to be artefacts of different origin. (EXP-R2-062.)
12. **A single declaration, imported, never reimplemented.** The rendering defect of §0.1 survived a withdrawal that fixed `src/transfer/arms.py` because `scripts/transfer_gap/tg_common.py` carried a second copy. Any module that decides what string a model is fed must import that decision from the panel declaration. (EXP-R2-028, EXP-R2-062.) The same holds one step earlier, for *which records exist* and *which arms a stage measures*: EXP-R2-063 found the latter maintained by hand in five places, two of which disagreed with `PANEL` — one stage's panel silently narrowed by an arm, another silently widened by one. Use `scripts/transfer/panel_contract.py::arm_can_run`. **A third instance, one level up, 2026-08-14 (EXP-R2-201):** the transcoder trainer's auxiliary-revival constant is the literal 192, which its own docstring identifies as the reference rule `min(d_model // 2, n_dead)` evaluated at `d_model` 384 — and it was applied unchanged on a backbone ten times wider, where that rule returns 2,048. A constant and the rule that defines it are two sources for one fact, and the derived one wins silently at every width but the one it was derived at. The repair is to derive the value rather than to change it, and it carries no claim of measured benefit: raising it to the rule's own value moved the statistic it was suspected of holding down by less than seed noise (§8 item 5).
13. **Declare a stage's cohort band against the band the arms were qualified on.** Four stages of one campaign draw protein cohorts on three different bands (64–246, 64–120, 600–2000), and the cohort `cohort_power` qualifies is not the cohort `lens_family` scores. A per-stage band is a legitimate compute choice; an undeclared one lets a PASS verdict be read as covering a population it was never measured on. (EXP-R2-063.)
14. **A scheduler must obey what a module can deliver, not what the panel intends.** `ArmSpec.capabilities` is an intent and a module's architecture declaration is a deliverable; they are allowed to disagree, and exactly one predicate should consult both. Handing a stage an arm it cannot serve does not fail cheaply — it fails after the checkpoint is on the GPU. (EXP-R2-060, EXP-R2-063.)
15. **A remote runner's success is not the transport's exit code.** Verify the channel carries failure before trusting a green campaign: this one does not, and returned 0 for a command that exited 7. A campaign's verdict must be decidable from the campaign's own output. (EXP-R2-068, L20.)
15b. **Measure a small causal effect in float32, not bfloat16.** A far-band
patching effect of order 0.05 logits cannot be read off a bfloat16 metric whose
quantisation step is 0.0625: the eligible fraction it produced was inflated by 60%
relative, upward. `14_paa_census.py` had already learned this on the M-gap and
defaults to float32 for it; `04_circuit_primitives.py` did not, and the same
lesson cost a second measurement. (EXP-R2-068.)

16. **A declaration derived by comparing resolved paths is not host-portable.** Two environment variables that are distinct on the authoring host can alias on the running host, and then the comparison silently picks whichever branch is written first. Declare the choice where it is made. (EXP-R2-068, L21.)

17. **Prefer threshold-free statistics.** Where a threshold is unavoidable, sweep
it and show the ordering is invariant across the sweep. Two results this
programme recorded as orderings turned out to hold only above a cut: the
far-band propagation ordering reverses at the two most permissive eligibility
thresholds, and the protein/text separation in EXP-R2-070 is clean at 0.50 and
31/40 at 0.05.

18. **Never delete a result artifact; move it.** Frozen provenance is external at
`/Data2/lzp/bio_archive` and is never edited in place. A zero-byte file from a
failed pull is not an artifact, but moving it costs nothing and records that the
pull path can fail silently.

19. **Check `nvidia-smi` and `free -h` before and after every GPU run**; on H200,
run them inside the selected pod. Allocation is not utilisation, and "the GPUs
were idle" must be distinguishable from "nobody looked".

20. **Report failures rather than routing around them.** A refutation is a valid
result; state it plainly. This includes one's own operational failures — three
were recorded in EXP-R2-070's log entry rather than smoothed over, and one of
them (a panel-scoped stage serialised onto a single GPU) would otherwise have
cost thirteen hours silently.

21. **Give every arm the same number of sampling units before comparing them, and
use a scale-free statistic when the arms differ in size.** An interval-overlap
test across arms carrying 288 and 128 cases is partly a reading of sample size,
and it supported a far-band claim that a matched-count re-run then narrowed. The
same error hides inside per-arm proportions: "the top 1% of heads" is one head on
GPT-2 and seven on ProtGPT2, so concentration must be read from a Gini or a
share-of-grid rather than a top-k share. (EXP-R2-070, EXP-R2-071.)

22. **Cohort sensitivity is a property of the statistic, not of the modality.**
Measured in one session on the same arms: the pathway-budget ratio reproduced
within 0.05 on every arm across draws, while the far-band eligible fraction moved
a quarter of its own value on ProtGPT2 between two windows of one permutation. A
draw-robustness result for one measurement licenses nothing about another, in
either direction. (EXP-R2-068, EXP-R2-069.)

23. **Never normalise a signed decomposition by summed magnitudes.** The four
causal-path components sum to the total exactly and still oppose in sign on
40–50% of heads, so a "share" built from summed absolute values is not a share of
anything — ProGen2-medium's reached 1.03 of the whole it was supposedly part of,
and the modality contrast it produced was an artefact of the normalisation.
Confirm the components do not cancel before dividing by them. (EXP-R2-071.)

24. **A statistic compared against the set that selected its own inputs is
circular, and the circular answer is indistinguishable from a real one.** A
top-20 Jaccard against the prefix-matching census returned 1.0 on all four arms
because the sender set *was* the census-selected set — which is exactly what
genuine agreement would return, so nothing in the artefact revealed the defect.
Enforce the precondition in code and raise; a comment does not stop the next run.
(D2.b, repaired in `causal_census_agreement`.)

25. **Before reading a low rank correlation as absence of signal, test reliability
and concentration.** Protein arms gave an all-head census-to-causal ρ near zero,
which was first recorded here as the census carrying no information about causal
rank. That was wrong: per-head reliability was high, so it was not attenuation,
and ρ over the top 32 heads was +0.808 on ProtGPT2 — higher than either text
control then on the panel. The bulk of the grid was unordered, not the top of it,
and the finding had to be retracted and restated. (EXP-R2-071.) *The supporting
numbers in this rule were themselves corrected by EXP-R2-072/073 — see §EXP-R2-071;
the reasoning is what the rule carries, not the figures.*

26. **Declare the unit of every distance, window and band, and resolve it per arm
when the arms differ in symbols per token.** The far-band propagation estimand was
declared in tokens and compared across arms at 4.4x, 2.8x and 1.0x symbols per
token. The ordering between the two *protein* arms — same content alphabet, only
the tokeniser differing — reverses between a token band and a residue band, so the
sign of the reported contrast was a free parameter of an unstated choice. The
artefact recorded `symbols_per_token` in the same file and nothing consumed it. A
band constant with no per-arm resolution is a cross-arm claim waiting to happen.
(EXP-R2-073.)

28. **A selector must be scored against the trivial baseline available from its
own coordinates.** A head-prevalence census was compared against chance for
months and never against *ranking heads by layer index alone*. Measured: that
baseline recovers 0 of the causal top-20 on gpt2-large against the census's 7, so
it is genuinely weak on text — and 5 of 6 on ProtGPT2, and 4 against the census's
1 on ProGen2-small. Two published readings changed on contact with it: a "pass"
that was substantially depth-carried, and a failure that is worse than chance
because it loses to the head's own coordinates. Chance is not the only free
baseline; anything computable from the unit's index without looking at the data is
free, and a selector that does not beat it has not been shown to work.
(EXP-R2-131.)

29. **A smoke run must not be written into the results tree, and its weight
payloads must not live on in the log tree.** A 12-sequence
interface check for a new arm was written to `results/transfer/`, and a later
audit reading only local artefacts found it, read it as the arm's measurement, and
reported the control at 1.9× its own chance against the campaign's 5.3×. The
campaign draws were on GPFS and had never been pulled, so nothing local
contradicted it. Smoke output goes to ignored `logs/smoke/`; a condition filter in
the reader is a second line of defence and not the first. (EXP-R2-131.) **The
retention half, added 2026-08-12 after the same directory grew to 255 MB of a
273 MB `logs/` tree, almost all of it trained dictionaries from four checks.** A
check keeps its report artefacts — the JSON, NPZ and TSV it wrote — because those
are the record that it ran and what it found; a model-weight payload goes once the
check's numbers have reached `docs/EXPERIMENT_LOG.md`, which is where they are
cited from anyway. There is no chokepoint to hook, because a smoke run is an
ad-hoc `--out logs/smoke/…` invocation of a stage entry point rather than a
scheduled item, so this half is enforced by discipline and is written as a rule
for exactly that reason.

27. **A statistic quoted across arms must state its denominator.** Per-head causal
effects are normalised by each arm's own clean-minus-corrupt metric, and those
denominators span 32x across the panel (0.76 to 24.10 logits). The same head read
15.7x the text control's on the normalised scale and 0.50x on the logit scale. A
normalised effect is a within-arm quantity unless the denominator travels with it.
(EXP-R2-072.)

30. **Derive a content mask from the rendering that produced the string, not from
the tokenizer's special-id list — and re-run a blast-radius survey rather than
restating one.** A ProGen2 arm's N-to-C control marker `1` is an ordinary
vocabulary entry — id 3, while the tokenizer reports `all_special_ids = [30]` —
so it passed the mask, entered the scored span, and therefore entered the
fully-ablated endpoint that is the *denominator* of every recovery ratio on that
lineage; excluding it removes exactly one position per cohort record.
**The displacement is large where it acts and small where it is read, and both
halves have to be reported.** Measured at 128 records on all four ProGen2 rungs,
the per-layer mean block-output norm — the anchor a relative perturbation is read
against — moves **−15.8% to −31.7% at layers 1 and 2 on every rung**, growing
mildly with depth, and `progen2-large` carries a **second affected band at layers
21–31 (−2.1% to −5.5%)** that a single-arm check at 32 records could not see, so
"layers 1 and 2, and nothing else" is a `progen2-small` statement rather than a
lineage one. The denominator those norms feed moves only **−0.49% to +0.84%**,
and in both directions, because the endpoint is a vector that one dropped position
swings a long way at layers 1–2 while the mean-ablated cross-entropy is a scalar
over the whole stack. **The general lesson: a relative-norm displacement of an
intermediate quantity is not a bound on the displacement of the statistic that
consumes it, in either direction.**
**The survey half is a coordination failure and is recorded as one.** The original
judgement that no published number could be affected was sound when it was made,
and its *restatement* at the repair was false when committed: twelve stage-23
ProGen2 artefacts had been written by another agent two and a half hours earlier,
in the same tree, and the repair asserted that none existed. Nothing had to be
withdrawn, and only because that ladder campaign had recorded no conclusion — which
is luck rather than process. A blast-radius statement is a measurement of the tree
as it stands, not a claim that can be carried forward.
**The re-run has landed, and it is a controlled replacement rather than a lucky
agreement (EXP-R2-183).** The mask feeds the denominator alone — the clean pass is
unmasked and the perturbation is applied at every position of every replaced block
— so clean and damage values are mask-independent by construction and the whole
correction is one scalar per cell. That is falsifiable and it was used as the
check: **all 76 epsilon points across the twelve cells reproduce their damage to a
maximum absolute difference of exactly 0.000e+00.** No qualitative reading moves —
largest recovery change anywhere **0.0069**, every superseded recovery value inside
the corresponding corrected 95% bootstrap interval with zero exceptions, and the
rung ordering identical before and after at every epsilon in all three conditions.
The twelve pre-fix cells were moved rather than deleted, under `_superseded/` with
READMEs naming the defect, the fixing commit, the provenance hashes and the
replacement (rule 18); two that had completed on the pod and never been pulled were
pulled first, so the superseded record is the complete twelve rather than the ten
that had reached local disk. **Any ProGen2 result from this stage must be
post-fix**, and supersession is now what enforces that rather than an instruction.
(EXP-R2-168, EXP-R2-170, EXP-R2-183.)

31. **A matched-configuration certificate must enumerate everything that changes
the object it certifies, and must be able to fail on what it names.** The pair
gate for two dictionaries omitted the whole optimisation block, so two artefacts a
**hundredfold apart in learning rate**, at different seeds and at batch 8 against
64, registered `MATCHED` with no disagreements. The failure sat one level below a
short list — the record carried no such field at all, so widening the field tuple
alone would have named fields that did not exist. Learning rate, weight decay,
gradient clip, batch size, the auxiliary-revival width and both seeds are now
matched; `steps` is not, because a budget-matched pair must be free to differ on
it. **No published number is affected: the gate postdates every F11 measurement**,
and the F11-era replacement artefacts carry no training block for it to have
certified. **One axis is reported rather than matched, and it is genuinely
unmatched in the live joint pilot**: `--max-tokens` is 512 for text against 1024
for protein, because a protein rendering pays a measured wrapper and that mode's
residue ceiling is derived from the cap, so forcing the two equal would either
truncate the protein corpus below its band or move the text window for no reason.
That pair therefore certifies `MATCHED` while its two dictionaries saw different
context lengths, and a reader of the verdict has to be able to see that without
opening a settings block. **Future joint pairs should run equal caps at 1024.**
(EXP-R2-170.)

32. **A fixture may not assert in prose a property its construction does not establish.** Twice in this lineage, independently, an end-to-end fixture's docstring vouched for the property the fixture destroyed. The diffing stage's protein corpus cycled four sequences under an appended tail and inferred from the records being *distinct* that "the two splits cannot overlap by content" — every sixteen were near-copies of one base, and distinctness is not independence; its text corpus carried the same shape, four documents differing by a suffix and sharing every word five-gram but one. The transcoder trainer's corpus cycled a four-record list over a whole shuffle block under a docstring stating that the arithmetic made its training and held-out sets disjoint — disjoint by index and identical by content. Both survived review by the same mechanism, and it is the mechanism rather than the property that generalises: a docstring is the first thing a reviewer reads and the last thing that can establish anything, so a contract stated in prose gets read as evidence in exactly the place the property should have been checked. Nothing here is specific to disjointness, which is only where it surfaced twice, or to protein corpora, since one of the four fixtures is text. **Constructively: state the contract, build the fixture so that the contract is checkable rather than asserted, and turn the broken construction into a negative path that must refuse.** Both repairs did this — a pool of one near-duplicate group and a corpus of one repeated record are now tests that the stage rejects them — so what used to be the fixture's norm is now an assertion that would fail. (EXP-R2-175, EXP-R2-191; the corpus measurement that makes these fixtures wrong is L30.)

33. **A criterion stated per unit must be evaluated per unit, and the instrument has to persist the per-unit vector.** D3.h-B2 required its condition at the layers a difference is reported on and was then read as a cross-layer mean, because the trainer recorded one dead-latent scalar per dictionary and nothing downstream could recover the layer axis. The two are not the same rule at different resolutions: a dictionary averaging 7,608 live latents per layer keeps 90 at one of them, so the mean and the criterion disagree in *verdict*. The collapse is invisible in the artefact, which is why it survived two readings of the gate. State the rule at one resolution, record the vector the rule needs, and pin the disagreeing case as a test — here, a dictionary whose mean clears the cut while one layer fails it must read FAIL. (EXP-R2-203, L32.)

34. **A pilot shorter than one evaluation interval certifies plumbing, not trainability.** EXP-R2-205 ran the Crosscoder end-to-end on the live pair, at campaign width, for 260 steps, and everything it reported was correct: tokenizer digest, 32L x 4096d comparability, the near-duplicate screen, the parameter count in closed form, the admissibility refusal firing at the right layer, and measured memory. What it could not report is the one quantity that decided the campaign. `--eval-every` is 2,000, so the first `active_fraction` reading does not exist until step 2,000 — and by then both campaign cells had already collapsed to 1.5% and 11.2% of the TopK budget they were supposed to fill, a state they never left. The pilot was 7.7x too short to see the failure, and nothing in its output hinted at one. **A pilot must run at least one full evaluation interval, and the quantity that decides whether the objective is trainable at all must appear in the pilot's own output rather than only in the campaign's.** For a TopK dictionary that quantity is the fraction of selected latents surviving the ReLU, whose healthy value is exactly `k / d_hidden` and is therefore checkable without a reference run. The general form: a pilot certifies the code path it exercised for the duration it ran, and "it started cleanly" is not evidence about an objective whose failure mode is a slow fixed point. (EXP-R2-205, EXP-R2-206, EXP-R2-208.)
35. **A peak location from a per-layer curve is reported with its prominence, or it is not reported.** State the peak over the cell's own interior median, the count of interior layers tying within 2% of the maximum, **and the same two figures for a null** — prominence is bounded below by 1 by construction and exceeds it on any finite sample of anything, so a bare value is a comparison with nothing. On this repository's spectra the null is `coordinate_independent_control` (excess 0.0014–0.0038 over six cells) and **not** `isotropic_control`, which is pinned at the estimator's ceiling of 4,026 at every layer of every cell and has no variance to calibrate against. Reading the wrong one returns a null of exactly 1.000 and makes everything look significant; a large tie count means the argmax is not identified and "the peak is at layer L" is not a statement. The instance: EXP-R2-202's four `r99` curves were quoted as showing the peak moving from layer 3 to layer 18 in protein and, in the opposite direction, from 26 to 2 in text. With prominence attached, `base/protein` is a genuine peak at 1.317x its median with **one** interior layer within 2%, and `stage1/protein` is a genuine peak at 1.150x that must be read as **the region 17–22** rather than as layer 18 exactly — its tie set is {17, 18, 19, 22}, and the lineage's third point (`ProLLaMA`, 1.162x, ties {17, 18, 19, 20, 21, 22}) fills the interior dip and confirms the region is broad. An earlier summary of this same measurement wrote it as the band 17–19 by dropping the tied layer 22, which is the tidying this rule exists to prevent and is recorded in the log rather than silently repaired. But `base/text` sits at **1.009x** its median with **23 of 30** interior layers tied within 2% — a flat line whose argmax is wherever noise left the maximum — and `stage1/text` is bimodal, its layer-2 maximum tied by layers 22 through 27. The protein shift survives; the text "crossover" was an artefact of picking one of two tied modes on each side and is withdrawn. This repository has several per-layer curves in play — `r99`, live latents, per-site NMSE, decoder penalty — and every one of them invites the same error, which is why this is a rule rather than a note on the measurement that produced it. (EXP-R2-202 and its prominence addendum.)
36. **An exit status reports the last process in the chain, not the work; only the work's own recorded status is evidence that it finished, and finishing is a different fact from succeeding.** This repository already applies the principle to the pod, where the access layer returns 0 whatever the remote command exits with (L20), so a launcher's success is not evidence about a stage and the stage's own output is. The same failure recurs indoors and in three distinct forms, all found in one session on the local test suite: a pipeline reports `tail`'s status rather than pytest's, so a red suite behind `| tail -3` is indistinguishable from a green one; a piped run emits nothing until it ends, so an interim read shows an empty log for a healthy run and for a dead one alike; and a run terminated with its parent leaves a plausible sequence of progress dots, no failure text, and no status line, which reads as success to anything that greps for failure. **Record the work's own exit code as the last line of its own output, gate on that line, and treat its absence as "did not finish" rather than as either outcome.** A watcher must fire both when the line appears and when the process disappears without it; one that waits only for the success marker is silent through exactly the failure it exists to catch. **And a status file needs exactly one writer.** The fourth instance was self-inflicted within an hour of validating the campaign queue's lock, which exists because "two runners on one status file would interleave two rewrites of it": a suite believed dead was still running, a second run was started onto the same path, and the `>` truncated the file under the first — after which neither run's status meant anything and the stalled byte count that prompted the relaunch was itself the buffering artefact above, not a dead process. Before relaunching anything onto a status path, check for a live writer and give the new run its own file. **The watcher itself must read the two conditions atomically**, and the fifth instance was the mirror image of the others: a monitor that tested "status line present?" and then "writer still alive?" as separate steps caught the window between the process exiting and its own status line landing, and declared a suite that had passed 1,340 tests to be unfinished. That error is in the safe direction -- it refuses a commit rather than admitting one -- but it is the same defect, and the fix is to re-read the status line after observing the process gone before concluding anything. (The operational recipe for the suite is in `docs/PROJECT_LOG.md`, 2026-08-17.)
